*Francesco Mazza,Marcello Caleffi,Angela Sara Cacciapuoti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Q2NSViz 是一个开源 Python 可视化工具，通过回放量子网络仿真轨迹，展示物理拓扑、飞行中量子比特、纠缠关系等动态演化，支持 JSON/NDJSON 格式，提供零编码的交互式可视化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络具有非经典特性，其仿真理解和直观分析固有困难，缺乏有效的可视化工具来弥合抽象协议逻辑与具体执行之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于解耦的 JSON/NDJSON 轨迹协议、Qt-free 回放引擎和 PyQt6 交互界面，重构仿真网络状态的时间演化，包括物理拓扑、存储/飞行中量子比特、经典比特/包、测量结果和纠缠关系。

Result: 实现了可导航、可复现的可视化工件，将执行轨迹转化为直观的可视化呈现，暴露出物理连接性和协议执行产生的动态纠缠诱导结构。

Conclusion: Q2NSViz 为研究人员和教育者提供了零编码工具，可独立用于 Q2NS 及其他兼容仿真后端，缩小了抽象协议逻辑与具体执行之间的差距。

Abstract: The unique and non-classical features of quantum networks make their simulation and intuitive understanding inherently difficult. In this work, we present Q2NSViz, an open-source Python-based visualization tool for replaying and inspecting quantum-network simulation traces. Q2NSViz reconstructs the time evolution of the simulated network state, including physical topology, stored and in-flight qubits, classical bits and packets, measurements, and entanglement relationships. In this way, it exposes not only physical connectivity, but also the dynamic entanglement-induced structure produced, consumed, and transformed by protocol execution. Q2NSViz is built around a decoupled JSON/NDJSON trace contract, a Qt-free replay engine, and an interactive PyQt6 interface, making it a standalone companion to Q2NS and reusable by other simulation backends that emit the same trace format. By turning execution traces into navigable and reproducible visual artifacts, Q2NSViz provides a zero-coding tool for researchers and educators, narrowing the gap between abstract protocol logic and concrete execution.

</details>


### [93] [Strain-Induced Detuning of a Dressed Nitrogen-Vacancy Qubit: Effective Two-Level Theory and Its Validity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12453)
*Jihyeon Jeon,Donghun Lee,Seok-Kyun Son,Nojoon Myoung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对金刚石中氮-空位（NV）中心微波缀饰量子比特，推导了考虑横向应变的解析有效二能级模型，揭示了应变导致缀饰态共振频率偏移和自旋锁定轴倾斜，从而丧失对静磁场的零阶不敏感性，并给出了实用化的有效性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想二能级极限下，微波缀饰NV量子比特的跃迁频率对静磁场一阶不敏感，具有抗磁噪声鲁棒性。然而实际金刚石器件中残余横向晶体应变会混合自旋子能级并改变缀饰量子比特，需要量化这种鲁棒性的损失。

Method: 通过微扰消去全三能级缀饰哈密顿量中的远失谐旁观态，推导出受应变缀饰NV量子比特的解析有效二能级模型；利用速率方程光读出的模拟脉冲电子自旋共振谱验证；从全三能级哈密顿量的本征值和旁观态权重导出精确有效性判据，并简化为两个可控准则。

Result: 获得了缀饰态分裂、自旋锁定混合角以及纵向磁场耦合的闭式表达式；证明横向应变使缀饰态共振频率偏移且自旋锁定轴倾斜，这两种效应恢复了有限的直流磁场响应，从而量化了磁鲁棒性的损失；提出了关于轴向场-横向应变平面的有效性相图。

Conclusion: 建立了包含应变的缀饰NV量子比特解析理论，给出了实用化的有效性判据和设计指南，为设计缀饰NV传感实验提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center in diamond can be operated as a microwave-dressed qubit. In the ideal two-level limit, its transition frequency is first-order insensitive to static magnetic fields, providing robustness against magnetic detuning noise. In practical diamond devices, however, residual transverse crystal strain mixes the $\ket{m_{s}=\pm1}$ spin sublevels and modifies the dressed qubit. In this study, we derive an analytical effective two-level model of a strained dressed NV qubit by perturbatively eliminating the far-detuned spectator state from the full three-level dressed Hamiltonian. We obtain closed-form expressions for the dressed-state splitting, the spin-locking mixing angle, and the longitudinal magnetic-field coupling. We show that transverse strain shifts the dressed-state resonance and tilts the spin-locking axis. These two effects restore a finite DC-field response and thereby quantify the loss of magnetic robustness. We demonstrate these features in simulated pulsed electron spin resonance spectra that incorporate rate-equation-based optical readout. We further derive exact validity criteria from the eigenvalues and spectator weights of the full three-level Hamiltonian. For practical use, we reduce these criteria to two controlled guidelines: the spectator-like branch must remain above the nominal upper dressed state, and its branch-specific admixture must remain small. A validity diagram over the axial-field--transverse-strain plane summarizes these approximate conditions and provides practical guidelines for designing dressed-NV sensing experiments.

</details>


### [94] [Sparse anisotropic positive maps for qutrit entanglement: exact indecomposability and PPT geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12470)
*Massimiliano F. Sacchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了三比特系统上稀疏双随机正映射的两参数族，完整解析了其正性、完全正性、可分解性、PPT纠缠态检测、最优性及暴露凸几何性质，构建了统一的解析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正映射是检测纠缠的重要工具，尤其是超越PPT准则的不可分解正映射。本文旨在提供一个可精确求解的理论框架，以便系统研究正映射与纠缠检测之间的深层几何结构。

Method: 引入了一个依赖于两个参数w和z的稀疏双随机正映射族，通过解析推导得到完整的相图：正性区域为[0,2/3]²，完全正性区域为[0,1/3]²，可分解性在w,z≥1/3区域外丢失。在端点W(2/3,2/3)处构造了秩类型为(5,5)的四参数PPT边缘态族，并推导了其解析检测区域。

Result: 完整刻画了该正映射族的相图边界；证明了不可分解区域可通过显式构造的PPT纠缠态认证；展示了W(2/3,2/3)处对应的射线是PPT锥的暴露面；给出了一个严格更优的优化映射，其检测区域更大。

Conclusion: 该工作为三比特系统中正性、不可分解性、PPT纠缠、最优性和暴露凸几何提供了可解析处理的统一研究框架，有助于深入理解正映射与纠缠检测的几何结构。

Abstract: Positive but not completely positive maps provide one of the most direct ways to detect entanglement beyond the positive-partial-transpose (PPT) criterion. We introduce and analyze an exactly solvable two-parameter family of sparse bistochastic positive maps on qutrits, in which two coherence channels are independently tuned by parameters $w$ and $z$. The sparse structure makes the full phase diagram analytic: positivity holds exactly on the square $0\le w,z\le2/3$, complete positivity on the smaller square $0\le w,z\le1/3$, and decomposability is lost precisely outside a quarter circle in the corner $w,z\ge1/3$. The indecomposable region is certified by explicit PPT entangled state adapted to the same witness geometry. At the endpoint $W_*=W(2/3,2/3)$ we construct a four-parameter family of PPT edge states of rank type $(5,5)$, derive their analytic detection region, and show that the corresponding rays are exposed faces of the PPT cone. Finally, although $W_*$ is not optimal, we give an explicit optimal refinement whose detection region on this family is strictly larger. The result is an analytically tractable qutrit setting in which positivity, indecomposability, PPT entanglement, optimality, and exposed convex geometry can be studied in a single framework.

</details>


### [95] [Benchmarking loss functions for trainable quantum feature maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12487)
*Nguyen Dinh Quyen,Vu Tuan Hai,Quoc Chuong Nguyen,Le Bin Ho,Lan Nguyen Tran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种损失函数（Log-Likelihood Loss、Distance Loss、Measurement Loss）在可训练量子特征映射（TQFM）中的优化动态、计算成本和分类性能，发现Log-Likelihood Loss在保持线性计算复杂度的同时，比Measurement Loss具有更稳定的优化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定量子特征映射对复杂非线性分类任务表达能力不足，而TQFM虽能增强学习能力，但不同损失函数对优化动态的影响尚未被充分理解，亟需系统比较以指导实践。

Method: 将Log-Likelihood Loss应用于TQFM，并与Distance Loss和Measurement Loss进行系统对比，通过大量数值实验分析三者的优化动态、计算成本和分类性能。

Result: Log-Likelihood Loss在保持线性计算复杂度的前提下，相比Measurement Loss实现了更稳定的优化过程。

Conclusion: 该基准研究为量子核优化中平衡可训练性、计算效率和预测性能提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Many quantum machine learning models employ quantum feature maps to encode classical data into quantum states. While fixed feature maps often lack sufficient expressivity for complex nonlinear classification tasks, trainable quantum feature maps (TQFMs) enable adaptive quantum kernels with enhanced learning capability. Different loss functions can induce distinct optimization dynamics, yet their effects remain poorly understood. In this work, we apply the Log-Likelihood Loss function for TQFMs and provide a systematic comparison with Distance Loss and Measurement Loss. Through extensive numerical experiments, we compare their optimization dynamics, computational costs, and classification performance. Our results show that Log-Likelihood Loss consistently achieves more stable optimization than Measurement Loss while retaining linear computational complexity. The resulting benchmark offers practical guidance for balancing trainability, computational efficiency, and predictive performance in quantum kernel optimization.

</details>


### [96] [The Quantum Polariton Hamiltonian that Reproduces the Same High-Harmonic Generation Spectra as the Classical Hamiltonian in Strong Laser Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12560)
*Nimrod Moiseyev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在红外泵浦光子数远大于发射紫外光子数的条件下，定义一个能产生与Floquet理论相同高次谐波产生（HHG）光谱的量子哈密顿量的可能性。核心假设是发射的高次谐波强度远低于红外激光强度，且第N次谐波的发射源于吸收N个红外光子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在特定条件下（红外泵浦光子数远大于发射紫外光子数），是否存在一个量子哈密顿量能够重现Floquet理论预测的HHG光谱，从而可能提供更简洁或更本质的理论描述。

Method: 基于两个关键假设进行理论推导：(1) 发射的高次谐波强度远低于红外激光强度；(2) 第N次谐波的发射源于吸收N个红外光子。

Result: 在给定假设条件下，理论上可能存在一个量子哈密顿量，其产生的HHG光谱与Floquet理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 该研究为高次谐波产生过程提供了新的理论视角，可能简化对强场物理中量子光学效应的描述。

Abstract: This manuscript investigates the possibility of defining a quantum Hamiltonian that leads to the same high-harmonic generation (HHG) spectra as those predicted by Floquet theory in the regime where the number of infrared (IR) pump photons is much larger than the number of emitted UV photons. The key assumption underlying our derivation is that the intensity of the emitted high harmonics is many orders of magnitude smaller than that of the IR laser, and that the emission of the Nnt harmonic results from the absorption of N IR photons.

</details>


### [97] [Fast measurement-based generation of large-scale Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger state with atomic nuclear-spin qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12565)
*Yan Lu,Xiao-Feng Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用碱土金属原子核自旋的量子铁磁门（QFG），通过快速测量引导的方案制备大规模GHZ态，理论上可扩展至243量子比特以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模GHZ态对量子技术至关重要，但制备困难，现有方法面临扩展性和保真度瓶颈。

Method: 利用碱土金属原子核自旋量子比特，通过里德伯缀饰实现四量子比特量子铁磁门（QFG）；采用“胶合电路”（一个QFG、两个单比特门及辅助比特投影测量）从乘积态逐步生成3、9、27、81、243…量子比特的GHZ态。

Result: 高保真QFG可在6π/Ωₘ时间内实现；基于当前技术分析，243量子比特GHZ态可行，且更多量子比特可通过更高检测保真度实现纠缠。

Conclusion: 该方法为可扩展、快速的测量引导型大规模GHZ态制备提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large-scale Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger~(GHZ) state is useful for quantum technologies but difficult to be prepared. Here, we propose fast measurement-based preparation of large-scale GHZ states by a four-qubit quantum phase gate with nuclear-spin qubits of alkaline-earth-like atoms, which is named as quantum ferromagnetic gate~(QFG) due to its analogy to the alignment of molecular magnetic moments in a classical magnet. A high-fidelity Rydberg-mediated QFG can be realized in a time of $6π/Ω_{\text{m}}$ with $Ω_{\text{m}}$ the maximal Rydberg Rabi frequency. From a product state of three data atom and one ancilla atom, a gluing circuit with one QFG, two single-qubit gates, and a projective measurement of the ancilla can generate a 3-qubit GHZ state, and repetition of this gluing circuit can lead to 9, 27, 81, 243, $\cdots$-qubit GHZ states. Analyses based on currently available techniques show that a 243-qubit GHZ state is realizable, and more qubits can be entangled with higher detection fidelity.

</details>


### [98] [Forked Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Non-Markovian Open Quantum Dynamics and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12567)
*Zhao-Wei Wang,Kai-Yu Yuan,Feng-Hua Ren,Zhao-Ming Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分叉物理信息神经网络（FPINN），通过梯度级解耦优化目标，实现了非马尔可夫开放量子系统的仿真与控制统一，在二量子比特海森堡XXX模型上验证了其超越传统方法的高保真度和平滑脉冲生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将量子系统仿真与控制解耦，且标准PINN在非马尔可夫系统中因多目标优化冲突而无法可靠收敛，亟需一种统一且稳定的端到端可微分框架。

Method: 在先前FPINN基础上引入专用控制分支，通过选择性梯度流在梯度层面解耦优化目标，将难以处理的多任务优化转化为良态条件数问题，实现仿真与控制的联合优化。

Result: 在二量子比特海森堡XXX模型上，FPINN准确再现了非马尔可夫动力学特征（退相干、信息回流）；在态制备任务中，保真度超越GRAPE、CRAB和标准PINN，且在耗散更强、更马尔可夫的环境中优势更显著；生成的脉冲更平滑，有利于实验实现。

Conclusion: 该框架为开放量子系统提供了一个统一的端到端可微分仿真与控制范式，对量子计算、量子仿真和量子控制具有潜在重要意义。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a pathway to reunify the simulation and control of quantum systems, in which these two tasks are typically decoupled in traditional strategies. However, most work remains confined to Markovian environments. When applied to non-Markovian systems, standard PINN architectures fail to converge reliably due to multi-objective optimization conflicts arising from the coupled differential equations. To address this fundamental limitation, we extend our previously proposed forked PINN (FPINN) by incorporating a dedicated control branch. By decoupling the optimization objectives at the gradient level via selective gradient flow, our method turns a previously intractable multi-task optimization into a well-conditioned one, allowing simulation and control to be optimized jointly without compromise. Numerical simulations on a two-qubit Heisenberg XXX model confirm that our framework faithfully reproduces the features of non-Markovian dynamics, including decoherence and information backflow. Taking a state-preparation task on the same model as an example, our FPINN achieves higher fidelity than gradient ascent pulse engineering, chopped random basis, and standard PINNs, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as the environment becomes more dissipative and more Markovian. The generated pulses are also noticeably smoother, which is advantageous for experimental implementation. Our framework thus provides a unified, end-to-end differentiable paradigm for simulation and control of open quantum systems, with potential implications for quantum computing, simulation, and control.

</details>


### [99] [Activating thermally charged quantum batteries in finite time: Thermodynamic trade-offs between correlations, work, and information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12573)
*Bhaskar Barman,Janine Splettstoesser,Henning Kirchberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过时间依赖耦合与辅助激活器相互作用的搅拌协议，将热充电的量子电池从被动态激活为有源态，从而实现按需能量提取。研究考虑了搅拌过程的能量成本、关联生成的熵成本及激活时间，推导了最大净可提取能量的界限，并通过投影测量进一步提升性能。在波导QED系统中以谐振子电池和二能级系统为例进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热充电量子电池能量稳定易得，但处于被动态，无法通过幺正操作按需提取有用能量，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 引入时间依赖的搅拌协议，通过时变耦合将电池与辅助激活器连接，生成关联驱动电池进入有源态；考虑搅拌能量成本、关联熵成本和激活时间，推导净可提取能量界限；引入投影测量利用信息增益进一步提升能量提取。

Result: 得出了最大净可提取能量（净遍历功）的界限；在波导QED系统中用谐振子电池和二能级系统验证了协议性能，表征了净可提取能量和输出功率。

Conclusion: 该搅拌协议可有效激活热充电量子电池，结合测量反馈可进一步提升性能，为量子电池的按需能量提取提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Thermally charged quantum batteries provide a stable and easy-to-have energy resource, but remain passive and therefore do not allow useful energy extraction on demand via unitary operations. We introduce a time-dependent stirring protocol that activates a thermally charged quantum battery through a time-dependent coupling to an auxiliary activator, thereby generating correlations that drive the battery into an active state. Accounting explicitly for the energetic cost of the stirring process, the entropic cost of correlation generation and the activation time, we derive bounds on the maximal net extractable energy. Incorporating projective measurement on the activator and exploiting the information gained through the measurement further enhances the extractable energy. As an experimentally relevant example, we analyze a waveguide-QED setup where a harmonic-oscillator battery (waveguide) is stirred and monitored by a two-level system. We characterize the performance of the protocol in terms of net extractable energy (net ergotropy) and power output.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum tests via inequalities for joint statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12591)
*Alfredo Luis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了违反即表明数据无法由联合概率分布描述的统计不等式，并检验了量子准概率分布、噪声联合测量对这些不等式的违反情况，发现最大混合态及所有系统态均存在违反，表明这不仅是系统态的性质，更是量子力学统计结构的固有属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计不等式（如Bell不等式）的违反通常被视为量子非定域性的证据，但本文希望更一般地检验是否存在一个共同的概率空间来描述量子数据，通过对更广泛的不等式的研究，探索量子力学中"联合概率分布不存在"这一现象的普遍性。

Method: 作者推导了一类统计不等式，其违反等价于数据无法由真实联合概率分布描述。随后将这些不等式应用于量子准概率分布（如Wigner函数等）以及包含噪声的联合测量所得的分布，测试它们是否违反这些不等式。

Result: 发现即使是最大混合态（通常被认为是经典态）也违反了这些不等式，并且在某些情况下所有量子态均违反。这表明该违反现象具有普遍性，不仅仅是特定量子态的量子非定域性体现。

Conclusion: 这些不等式的违反并不局限于特定的非经典态，而是反映了量子力学统计结构本身的根本特征——即量子理论中不存在一个适用于所有可观测量的全局联合概率空间，这是量子统计学相较于经典概率论的深层差异。

Abstract: In this work we derive statistical inequalities whose violation is equivalent to the impossibility of describing the data by a genuine joint probability distribution. So they are witnesses of the failure of a common probability space. We examine whether the most significant quantum quasidistributions violate these inequalities. We examine also whether these inequalitites are violated by the joint distributions derived form a noisy joint measurement. As a relevant example we find violations for the maximally mixed state, as well as cases where all system states violate them. This points to the idea that these results are more than a property of the system states, but are instead a property of the statistical structure of quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [101] [Constructing mode-resolved quantum optical models for emitters in photonic crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12603)
*Antonio Morales-Pérez,Iñaki García-Elcano,Chiara Devescovi,Maia G. Vergniory,Aitzol García-Etxarri,Alejandro González-Tudela*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种构建量子光学晶格模型的框架，该框架结合对称性约束的紧束缚构造与数值计算的光子能带结构及场分布，得到最小化、对称性强制的晶格哈密顿量，既能复现目标光子色散，又能保留光-物质耦合的位置和偏振依赖结构。该方法在微扰区域恢复基于格林函数的发射体动力学，同时支持非微扰量子动力学模拟，并在二维光子晶体中展示了标量模型无法实现的偏振相关定向发射及非马尔可夫动力学与纠缠分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电磁学方法（基于麦克斯韦方程求解器）能提供真实的矢量描述，但难以与量子多体和非微扰方法集成；而简化的量子光学格点模型虽易处理，但通常使用标量且空间无关的光-物质耦合，遗漏了结构化光子环境的关键特征。两者之间存在持久的权衡。

Method: 提出一个构造性框架：将对称性约束的紧束缚构造与数值计算的光子能带结构和场分布相结合，导出最小化、对称性强制的晶格哈密顿量，使其既能复现目标光子色散，又能保留模式分辨的（位置和偏振依赖的）光-物质耦合结构。

Result: 在微扰区域，该模型恢复基于格林函数的发射体动力学；同时支持非微扰量子动力学模拟。在二维光子晶体中，该方法捕捉到标量模型无法实现的偏振相关定向发射，并支持非马尔可夫光-物质动力学和纠缠分析。

Conclusion: 该结果在经典电磁模拟工具与量子光学多体及非马尔可夫建模之间建立了实用的桥梁。

Abstract: Recent advances are enabling quantum emitters to interact with photonic crystals, whose electromagnetic modes exhibit complex dispersion relations, spatial mode structure, and polarization textures. However, modeling light-matter behavior in these systems faces a persistent trade-off: electromagnetic approaches based on Maxwell-equation solvers provide realistic vectorial descriptions but are difficult to integrate with quantum many-body and non-perturbative methods, whereas simplified quantum-optical lattice models are tractable but typically rely on scalar and spatially independent light-matter couplings that miss essential features of these structured photonic environments. Here, we introduce a constructive framework to derive quantum-optical lattice descriptions that overcome this trade-off. Combining symmetry-constrained tight-binding constructions with numerically computed photonic band structures and field profiles, our method yields minimal, symmetry-enforced lattice Hamiltonians that reproduce the target photonic dispersion while retaining the mode-resolved (position- and polarization-dependent) structure of the light-matter coupling. We show that these models recover Green's-function-based emitter dynamics in the perturbative regime, while providing access to non-perturbative quantum dynamical simulations beyond emitter-only descriptions. As a proof of principle, we apply the framework to a two-dimensional photonic crystal and show that it captures polarization-dependent directional emission inaccessible to scalar models, while enabling the analysis of non-Markovian light-matter dynamics and entanglement. Our results provide a practical bridge between classical electromagnetic simulation tools and quantum-optical many-body and non-Markovian modeling in photonic crystal settings.

</details>


### [102] [James-Stein estimation for quantum sensing schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12651)
*Luke Alexander Rhodes,Sean William Moore,Jacob A. Dunningham*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨在有限数据量子计量学中，当缺乏未知参数的先验分布时，James-Stein估计器相较于标准技术能够提供显著优势，且无需任何先验信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子计量学协议通常优先考虑态制备、演化和测量这些量子环节，而数据分析被视为附加步骤，默认标准统计技术适用。然而在有限数据场景下，数据分析方法和估计器的代价函数选择至关重要，且贝叶斯分析需要可靠的先验分布。当没有可用先验时，需要新的估计方法。

Method: 将James-Stein估计器应用于有限数据的量子计量学方案中，该方法无需先验分布，能够同时对多个未知参数进行估计，并通过简单量子计量学方案进行优势验证。

Result: James-Stein估计器在测量多个未知参数且数据有限的场景下，相较于标准估计技术表现出显著优势，且不依赖任何先验分布信息。

Conclusion: 在有限数据量子计量学中，当缺乏可靠先验分布时，James-Stein估计器是一种有效的替代方案，能够在多参数估计中提供性能优势。

Abstract: Quantum metrology protocols typically consist of four steps: state preparation, evolution, measurement, and data processing. Often, the first three steps are prioritised when designing a scheme as they contain all the quantum elements. The data analysis is generally considered an add-on with an implicit assumption that this step is well behaved and so standard data techniques can be applied. However, the situation can be more nuanced, such as when the available data are limited. In limited-data quantum metrology the choice of data analysis technique and cost function of the estimator is of great importance, and a reliable prior distribution of the unknown parameters is required for Bayesian analysis. An interesting question is what we should do when no such prior is available. In this work, we consider how the James-Stein estimator can give significant advantages when measuring multiple unknown parameters with limited data and, importantly, does not require any prior distribution. We demonstrate the advantage by applying this methodology to simple quantum metrology schemes.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum metrology with undetected mid-infrared photons for applied non-destructive testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12653)
*Paul Gattinger,Andreas W. Schell,Maja Buchegger,Markus Brandstetter,Ivan Zorin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于未探测光子量子非线性干涉仪的中红外光谱学、显微成像和光学相干断层扫描技术，利用SPDC过程产生的纠缠光子对实现低功率、室温操作和散粒噪声极限检测，并与经典系统进行了对比和展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用量子效应和光子关联，通过可见/近红外域的测量获取中红外域的目标信息，实现超低探测功率、室温操作和散粒噪声极限检测，推动量子技术在实际非破坏性检测中的应用。

Method: 基于自发参量下转换（SPDC）构建量子非线性干涉仪，利用非简并关联信号光子和闲置光子分别作为探测光和探测光，实现未探测光子传感。

Result: 成功应用中红外光谱学、中红外显微成像和中红外光学相干断层扫描（OCT），并进行了系统表征和与经典系统的基准对比。

Conclusion: 未探测光子量子传感技术在中红外非破坏性检测领域具有重要前景，能够实现超低功率、室温操作等优势，是量子技术走向实际应用的 promising 方向。

Abstract: Metrology with undetected photons is an emerging technique that leverages quantum effects and photon correlations (entanglement) to retrieve valuable information in a target spectral range (e.g., mid-infrared, mid-IR) using measurements in an easily accessible domain (e.g., visible, near-IR). The underlying quantum process of spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC) is utilized to generate non-degenerate correlated signal and idler photons to serve as detection and probing photons, respectively. Sensing with undetected photons enables important advantages, such as ultra-low probe powers, room-temperature operation, and shot-noise-limited detection. In this contribution, we apply a quantum nonlinear interferometer based on an SPDC source to perform applied mid-IR spectroscopy, mid-IR microscopy, and mid-IR optical coherence tomography (OCT) as among the most promising techniques for quantum-based routine non-destructive testing. Moreover, we characterize the system, benchmark it against classical systems, and provide a prospective outlook for this new technology.

</details>


### [104] [Entanglement and Optical Nonreciprocity in spontaneous Raman Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12676)
*Frank Schlawin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了拉曼散射的微观量子理论，揭示了其与纠缠光子产生的联系，并发现拉曼散射产生的斯托克斯和反斯托克斯边带之间存在手性耦合，可实现非互易放大，为基于拉曼的量子光子学和量子增强光谱成像提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管非弹性光散射是物理和生命科学中样本表征和无标记成像的核心工具，且近期研究表明散射光可展现非经典关联，但拉曼散射产生量子关联的微观机制尚不清晰，缺乏与已建立的三波和四波混频纠缠光子产生理论的自然连接。

Method: 发展了一种拉曼散射的微观理论，使用累积量展开方法分析散射边带的纠缠结构，并与传统非共振非线性波混频进行比较。

Result: 发现拉曼散射在斯托克斯和反斯托克斯边带之间诱导出手性耦合，导致相干种子场的非互易放大，并且这些关联特征与传统的非共振非线性波混频产生的关联存在可区分的签名。

Conclusion: 建立的量子理论框架为基于拉曼的量子光子学协议提供了理论基础，并为实现量子增强的拉曼光谱学和成像指明了路径。

Abstract: Inelastic light scattering is a central tool for sample characterization and label-free imaging across the physical and life sciences. Recent work has suggested that scattered light can also exhibit nonclassical correlations. Here, we develop a microscopic theory of Raman scattering that connects naturally to established descriptions of entangled-photon generation in three- and four-wave mixing, while capturing essential differences arising from the resonant, dissipative character of Raman scattering. Using a cumulant-expansion approach, we analyze the entanglement structure of the scattered sidebands and identify signatures that distinguish Raman-mediated correlations from those generated in conventional off-resonant nonlinear wave mixing. In particular, we show that Raman scattering induces chiral couplings between Stokes and anti-Stokes sidebands, leading to nonreciprocal amplification of coherent seed fields. These results establish a theoretical framework for Raman-based quantum photonic protocols and suggest routes toward quantum-enhanced Raman spectroscopy and imaging.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum PDE Solvers in Practice: Application-Driven Benchmarking of the Heat Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12688)
*Mahmoud Elkarargy,Abdelaziz Rahwan,Abdelrahman Elsayed,Forat Hatem*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对1-D Dirichlet热方程，在统一问题实例和读取合约下，系统比较了11种量子PDE求解器（涵盖HHL、QSVT、VQLS、QITE、虚时演化、实时哈密顿模拟、酉膨胀及谱量子模拟等方法），在状态向量、理想采样（每步10^5次）和含噪Aer三种后端下评估了算法、采样和器件噪声误差。结果表明，QSM和Schade-Hamiltonian达到浮点精度，薛定谔化误差约10^-4，QITE对平滑数据表现最佳；固定采样下HHL相对ℓ2误差约0.79；范数失配归因于重建归一化；紧凑观测量比全场重建节省1-3个数量级采样。该基准为量子PDE求解器的实际选择提供了实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子PDE求解器研究使用的离散化方案、输出模型、重建规则和硬件假设各不相同，导致实际评估困难。本文旨在提出一个可复现的、应用驱动的基准测试框架，在统一条件下公平比较不同方法的性能表现。

Method: 以1-D Dirichlet热方程为基准问题，选取11种代表性量子核方法，使用三种初始条件、四种网格尺寸（n=4至7量子比特，N=16至128）、CFL比r≈0.4和终时T=1。在状态向量、理想采样（每步10^5次）和含噪Aer三种后端下分别分离算法误差、采样误差和器件噪声误差，并进行了范数失配消融分析。

Result: 状态向量下，QSM和Schade-Hamiltonian达到浮点精度，薛定谔化误差约10^-4，QITE对平滑数据最优；固定采样下HHL退化至约0.79相对ℓ2误差；范数失配消融显示哈密顿模拟、AVQDS和QLS-Fourier在n=7平滑初始条件下23-29%误差来自重建归一化；紧凑观测量比全场重建节省1-3个数量级采样。

Conclusion: 该基准为量子PDE求解器的实践选择提供了系统性的指南，揭示了不同方法在不同误差来源下的性能特征，以及采样效率与输出观测量的权衡关系。

Abstract: Quantum PDE solvers are difficult to evaluate in practice because published studies use different discretizations, output models, reconstruction rules, and hardware assumptions. We present a reproducible, application-driven benchmark for the 1-D Dirichlet heat equation that compares eleven kernels under the same problem instances and readout contract. The benchmark covers coherent linear solvers (HHL, QSVT, and QLS-Fourier), VQLS, imaginary-time methods (QITE, var-QITE, and AVQDS), real-time Hamiltonian simulation and unitary dilations (Hamiltonian simulation, Schade-Hamiltonian, and Schr"odingerisation), and the spectral quantum simulation method (QSM). We use three initial conditions, four grid sizes from $n=4$ to $7$ qubits ($N=16$ to $128$), a CFL-like ratio $r\approx0.4$, and final time $T=1$. Statevector, ideal-shot ($10^5$ shots per step), and noisy Aer backends separate algorithmic, sampling, and device-noise errors. On statevector, QSM and Schade-Hamiltonian reproduce the semi-discrete reference to floating-point precision, Schr"odingerisation reaches approximately $10^{-4}$ error, and QITE is the strongest non-transform method for smooth data. Under the fixed-shot setting, HHL degrades to approximately $0.79$ relative $\ell_2$ error, while several low-depth or postselected methods become readout-limited. A norm-mismatch ablation attributes 23--29% of the $n=7$ smooth-initial-condition error of Hamiltonian simulation, AVQDS, and QLS-Fourier to reconstruction normalization. Compact observables, including total thermal energy and individual Fourier-mode weights, require 1--3 orders of magnitude fewer shots than full-field reconstruction. The resulting public benchmark provides a practical guide for selecting quantum PDE solvers.

</details>


### [106] [When Close Enough Is Not Enough: Autoregressive Drift in Quantum Circuit Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12780)
*Mehdi Saeedi,Eddie Richter,Paul Hartke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer在容错量子计算电路优化中的应用，发现在允许后处理的参数化电路上效果优异（中位保真度1.000），但在需要精确离散等价性的Clifford+T电路中，由于自回归解码漂移问题，精确等价率随目标长度增加急剧下降（从88%降至接近0%），并通过推理时搜索和数据扩展部分缓解（最高39.5%），但训练侧微调和模型多样化无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算中，电路优化需在保持精确功能等价的同时最小化昂贵的非Clifford资源（如T门），传统方法难以高效解决该组合优化问题，因此探索使用Transformer模型进行电路优化。

Method: 使用44.8M参数的编码器-解码器Transformer，结构化电路令牌化，分别在参数化电路（2-6量子比特）和Clifford+T电路（3-6量子比特）上评估。参数化电路采用混合方法（Transformer输出结构+经典优化角度），Clifford+T电路直接生成离散门序列。

Result: 参数化电路上混合方法中位保龄度达1.000；Clifford+T电路上模型学习到有效语法和准确T门计数，但精确等价率随目标长度增加急剧下降（≤9门时88%，>26门时接近0%）。推理时搜索（多候选+等价验证）提升至22.5%，数据扩展2.5倍提升至39.5%，但长电路上仍低于4%。

Conclusion: 核心发现是Transformer在允许后处理近似输出的场景下成功，但在需要精确离散正确性时，自回归漂移限制了可靠性。推理时搜索和数据扩展是有效杠杆，而训练侧微调和模型多样化无效。

Abstract: Quantum circuit optimization for fault-tolerant computing requires exact functional equivalence while minimizing expensive non-Clifford resources such as T gates. We study this problem using a compact 44.8M-parameter encoder-decoder transformer with structured circuit tokenization, evaluating on parameterized circuits (2-6 qubits) and Clifford+T circuits (3-6 qubits). On parameterized circuits, a hybrid approach -- structure from the transformer, angles from classical optimization -- achieves median fidelity 1.000 on 3-6 qubit circuits. On Clifford+T circuits, where all gates are discrete and no post-processing is possible, the model learns valid syntax and accurate T-Count statistics, yet exact equivalence degrades sharply with target length -- from 88% on circuits with <=9 gates to near zero beyond 26 gates.
  We trace this failure to autoregressive drift: early-token divergence cascading irrecoverably through left-to-right decoding. Two levers partially mitigate the drift: inference-time strategies that generate multiple candidates and select via equivalence verification raise exact-match rates from 7% to 22.5%, while scaling training data by 2.5x pushes them to 39.5%. Yet the degradation with target length persists -- even with more data, exact equivalence drops from 94% on short circuits to under 4% beyond 26 gates.
  The contrast between settings is our central finding: when approximate outputs can be rescued by post-processing, the transformer succeeds; when exact discrete correctness is required, autoregressive drift limits reliability, with both inference-time search and data scaling as effective levers while training-side fine-tuning and model-level diversification are not.

</details>


### [107] [A Variational Surrogate Approach to Finite-Horizon Quantum Control via Hardware-Efficient Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12802)
*Nahid Binandeh Dehaghani,Rafal Wisniewski,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于硬件高效变分量子电路的有限时域量子控制框架，通过参数化量子电路替代传统时间相关控制场，以终端保真度为优化目标，实现多量子比特状态的高保真转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子控制方法需要显式合成时间相关控制场或施加哈密顿量可达性约束，过程复杂且对硬件依赖性强。本文旨在提供一种灵活、易于实现、与近期量子设备兼容的量子控制方案。

Method: 将控制目标重构为变分优化问题：使用硬件高效参数化量子电路（交替单量子比特旋转门和纠缠门层）作为终端演化的替代参数化，通过经典优化算法最小化终端不保真度。

Result: 在多量子比特状态转移基准测试上的数值实验表明，该方法能够实现高保真度状态转移，同时揭示了ansatz表达能力、优化复杂度与系统规模及电路深度之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该变分量子控制框架避免了问题特定或物理启发的ansatz依赖，为近期量子设备上的有限时域量子控制提供了一种灵活且易于实现的方案。

Abstract: We present a variational quantum framework for finite-horizon quantum control based on hardware-efficient ansätze. The objective is to steer a quantum system from a given initial state to a desired target state over a fixed time horizon by minimizing a terminal cost defined in terms of state fidelity. Instead of explicitly synthesizing time-dependent control fields or enforcing Hamiltonian reachability constraints, the proposed method reformulates the control objective as a variational optimization problem in which a hardware-efficient parameterized quantum circuit provides a surrogate parameterization of the terminal evolution. The circuit consists of alternating layers of single-qubit rotations and entangling gates, whose parameters are optimized using classical routines to minimize the terminal infidelity. This formulation avoids reliance on problem-specific or physics-inspired ansätze, providing a flexible and implementation-friendly approach compatible with near-term quantum devices. Numerical experiments on multi-qubit state-transfer benchmarks demonstrate high-fidelity state transfer while highlighting the trade-off between ansatz expressivity, optimization complexity, and scalability with respect to system size and circuit depth.

</details>


### [108] [A Quantum Computing Approach to Track Reconstruction in Strip-Type Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12821)
*Seungyeob Jwa,Hyunyong Kim,Jangho Kim,Minseok Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用量子退火进行条状气体探测器中的粒子径迹重建，将两个子问题公式化为QUBO问题，并在低堆积的DAMSA探测器事件中测试，结果表明QUBO公式能重现局部重建决策，为量子和经典计算结合的径迹重建方法提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在条状气体探测器中，鬼影击中点和多击中组合会使模式识别变为组合优化问题，传统方法难以高效处理，因此需要探索量子退火方法来解决这一挑战。

Method: 将两个重建子问题公式化为二次无约束二元优化问题：第一个子问题在局部候选区域内选择与单光子径迹相关的探测器击中点；第二个子问题从不同探测器层选择簇三重态，以在单次QPU提交中处理多条径迹候选。使用模拟的DAMSA探测器事件进行测试。

Result: 在单径迹击中点选择任务中，QPU重建的位置和角度分辨率接近基于Kalman滤波的重建结果。在多径迹关联任务中，从QPU样本中提取有效簇三重态，再通过基于图连通性的关联规则连接成径迹候选。在低堆积、以ALP衰变双光子信号为主的DAMSA事件中，QUBO公式能重现局部重建决策。

Conclusion: 该研究为在更复杂径迹环境中结合量子与经典计算的重建方法提供了实践基础。

Abstract: This study investigates the use of quantum annealing for particle track reconstruction in strip-type gaseous detectors. In such detectors, ghost hits and multiple hit combinations can turn pattern recognition into a combinatorial optimization problem. We formulate two reconstruction subproblems as quadratic unconstrained binary optimization problems. The first subproblem selects detector hits associated with a single photon track inside a localized candidate region. The second subproblem selects cluster triplets from different detector layers so that multiple track candidates can be handled within a single quantum processing unit(QPU) submission. The proposed formulations are tested using simulated DAMSA detector events. For the single track hit selection task, the QPU based reconstruction gives position and angular resolutions close to those obtained with a Kalman based reconstruction. In the simultaneous association task, valid cluster triplets are first extracted from the QPU samples and then connected using an association rule based on graph connectivity to construct track candidates. The DAMSA event topology studied here has low pileup and is dominated by the two photon signal from axion-like particle(ALP) decay. In this setting, the results show that the QUBO formulations can reproduce local reconstruction decisions. This provides a practical basis for further studies of reconstruction methods that combine quantum and classical computing in more complex tracking environments.

</details>


### [109] [The Resolution of the Identity as a Generator of Exact Integral Identities: A Coherent-State Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12822)
*Ghasem Asadi Cordshooli,Mehdi Mirzaee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Glauber相干态的完备性关系重新诠释为精确复高斯积分恒等式的直接生成元，通过将连续相干态投影到离散Fock基上，自然导出广义高斯积分关系，并揭示了过完备与严格正交表示的结构差异，以及参数极限中的非对易行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子力学教科书将"单位分解"仅作为被动表示工具，本文旨在重新审视这一视角，将其提升为主动生成数学恒等式的形式框架，并揭示希尔伯特空间完备性如何内在地编码大量精确可解的数学关系。

Method: 将Glauber相干态的连续完备性关系投影到离散Fock基上，系统推导广义高斯积分恒等式；通过对比过完备（相干态）与严格正交（Fock基）表示在投影下产生的离散Kronecker delta与连续Dirac delta定位核，揭示结构差异；分析主恒等式中参数极限的非对易行为。

Result: 获得了高度广义化的高斯积分恒等式族；证明了完备性关系在适当基投影下同时决定了离散与连续定位核；发现了主恒等式中参数极限取值的非对易性这一关键结构特征。

Conclusion: 该方法仅需相干态基本性质和Dirac形式体系，提供了直观透明的教学视角，展示了希尔伯特空间完备性内在地编码大量精确可解数学关系，适用于高年级本科生和研究生教学。

Abstract: The resolution of the identity is typically utilized as a passive representation tool in standard quantum mechanics textbooks. In this paper, we reinterpret this traditional perspective by introducing the resolution of the identity associated with Glauber coherent states as a direct generator of exact complex Gaussian integral identities. By systematically projecting the continuous coherent-state completeness relation onto the discrete Fock basis, highly generalized Gaussian integral relations emerge naturally as a direct consequence of state preservation, rather than being introduced as independent mathematical postulates. We demonstrate that this overarching formal structure simultaneously dictates both discrete (Kronecker delta) and continuous (Dirac delta) localization kernels under appropriate basis projections, providing a sharp conceptual contrast between the structural features of overcomplete and strictly orthogonal representations. Crucially, we highlight an intriguing non-commuting behavior in the parameter limits of the master identity. Requiring only the elementary properties of coherent states and the Dirac formalism, this approach offers a transparent and visually intuitive illustration of how Hilbert-space completeness inherently encodes vast libraries of exact, solvable mathematical relations -- providing a unifying perspective suitable for advanced undergraduate and graduate instruction.

</details>


### [110] [Detecting Phishing in Ethereum Networks using Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12828)
*Sai Sakunthala Guddanti,Anupama Ray,Mrunal Arun Kumavat,Anil Prabhakar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出混合量子-经典集成框架，结合QSVM和VQC检测以太坊交易中的欺诈行为，使用级联QRAC编码提升性能，在IBM Heron量子处理器上取得接近模拟器的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习方法虽优于统计方法但仍不及经典深度学习，为弥合这一差距，作者探索混合量子-经典集成方案的可行性。

Method: 采用混合量子-经典集成框架，结合QSVM和VQC；使用级联QRAC编码方案替代传统ZZ特征映射，在模拟器和IBM Heron量子处理器上进行对比实验。

Result: QRAC-VQC提升13%，QRAC-QSVM提升3%；QSVM在集成中保持最低假阴性和最高召回率；部分QML算法在高量子体积设备上接近模拟器性能。

Conclusion: 混合量子-经典集成框架能有效提升金融欺诈检测性能，QRAC编码优于ZZ特征映射，量子硬件在足够量子体积下具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This article explores the potential of Quantum Machine Learning (QML), specifically assessing a Quantum Support Vector Machine (QSVM) and a Variational Quantum Classifier (VQC) for detecting anomalies in real-world financial transaction data. While these QML methods outperform statistical methods, they fall short of cutting-edge deep learning techniques. To bridge this gap, we propose a hybrid quantum-classical ensemble framework that leverages the strengths of both domains. We demonstrate its effectiveness in detecting phishing in Ethereum transaction networks by combining complementary algorithms. The QSVM, whether used individually or in an ensemble, consistently delivered the lowest false negatives and higher recall rates, that are crucial for anomaly detection. To enhance individual models, we encoded the data using novel cascaded Quantum Random Access Coding (QRAC) schemes and compared it with the popular encoding ZZ feature map on both simulators and the IBM Heron quantum processor. For both QSVM and VQC, we consistently observed improvements (13% for QRAC-VQC and 3% for QRAC-QSVM) of QRAC over the ZZ feature map. Notably, certain QML algorithms exhibit remarkable resilience on the IBM Heron quantum processor, approaching simulator-level performance on devices with high quantum volume. This observation underscores the promise of QML despite hardware limitations.

</details>


### [111] [End-to-End Quantum Key Distribution Across Hybrid Fiber and Free-Space Links with All-Optical Encoding Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12837)
*Khen Cohen,Tomer Nahum,Michael Tzukran,Paz Or,Yehuda Pilnyak,Nitzan Livneh,Hagai Eisenberg,Yaron Oz,Haim Suchowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文演示了一种混合光纤与自由空间量子密钥分发(QKD)链路，通过在光学域内进行时间比特与偏振态之间的编码转换，实现了端到端的连续安全密钥生成，验证了编码转换器作为非可信量子信道一部分的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来量子网络需要桥接光纤（时间比特编码）和自由空间（偏振编码）两种不同光子编码的链路，当前的方案通常需要可信中间节点进行测量和重建，这破坏了量子信道的不信任假设。因此，需要一种无需测量或状态重建的全光学编码转换方法。

Method: 采用1550 nm的诱骗态BB84协议，设计并实现了全光学域的时间比特-偏振（T2P）和偏振-时间比特（P2T）转换器，在90米户外自由空间链路上进行连续安全密钥生成实验，并在750米自由空间扩展链路上验证单光子级操作。

Result: 在跨越两个数量级的大气湍流条件下（强白天湍流到静夜条件），链路保持会话平均量子比特误码率(QBER)为5.6-6.8%，远低于11%的BB84安全阈值；验证了750米自由空间扩展链路的单光子级操作。

Conclusion: 安全的光子编码转换可作为光纤与自由空间量子通信平台之间的实用接口，编码转换器作为非可信量子信道的一部分而非可信中间节点，为未来量子网络应用提供了基础构建模块。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) promises information-theoretically secure communication, but future networks must bridge fiber and free-space links that naturally employ different photonic encodings, namely time-bin in fiber and polarization in free space. Here we demonstrate a complete hybrid fiber and free-space QKD link that bridges both media within a single end-to-end protocol, converting between the two encodings entirely in the optical domain. Using the decoy-state BB84 protocol operating at 1550 nm, we demonstrate continuous secure-key generation over a 90 m outdoor free-space link. The system operates across atmospheric conditions spanning more than two orders of magnitude in the refractive-index structure parameter Cn^2, from strong daytime turbulence to quiescent nighttime conditions, and we further validate photon-level operation over a 750 m free-space extension. Throughout, the link maintains a session-mean quantum bit error rate (QBER) of 5.6-6.8%, well below the 11% BB84 security threshold. The encoding conversion is performed entirely in the optical domain without measurement or state reconstruction, preserving the security assumptions of the BB84 protocol. Consequently, the time-bin-to-polarization (T2P) and polarization-to-time-bin (P2T) converters remain part of the untrusted quantum channel rather than trusted intermediate nodes. These results establish secure photonic encoding conversion as a practical interface between fiber and free-space quantum communication platforms, providing a building block for future quantum networks applications.

</details>


### [112] [Thermal Suppression of Dynamical Quantum Phase Transitions in Finite-Dimensional Systems A Quasi-Hermitian Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12838)
*Jia-Chen Tang,Xu-Yang Hou,Ming-Zhang Wang,Hao Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在赝厄米框架下从第一性原理推导了有限温度猝灭动力学的混合态Loschmidt振幅，研究了有限维系统中热平衡态的动态量子相变。通过对一个N能级模型的分析，发现温度通过热权重在能级间的重新分布控制DQPTs，当希尔伯特空间维度达到5时出现有限阈值温度，超过该温度后Loschmidt振幅失去所有实零点，DQPTs被完全抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DQPTs研究多关注纯态或零温情况，对有限温度下热平衡态猝灭后的DQPTs缺乏系统的理论框架。本文旨在建立自洽的混合态赝厄米框架，并揭示温度如何通过热占据机制调控动态临界行为。

Method: 采用度量-平稳赝厄米框架从第一性原理构造混合态Loschmidt振幅；提出一个N能级模型（两能级扇区耦合N-2个旁观态），通过解析分析热权重分布与Loschmidt振幅零点行为的关系，研究温度对DQPTs的调控机制。

Result: 温度通过热权重在能级间的重新分布控制DQPTs；存在依赖于希尔伯特空间维度的阈值温度，当维度达到5时阈值温度有限，超过该温度后Loschmidt振幅失去所有实零点，DQPTs被完全抑制。

Conclusion: 热抑制机制提供了通过热占据控制动态临界性的一般原理，赝厄米框架为严谨推导该机制提供了自洽基础。该发现对理解有限温度下量子系统的非平衡动力学具有重要意义。

Abstract: We investigate dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPTs) in finite-dimensional systems prepared in thermal equilibrium states and subjected to a sudden quench. A mixed-state Loschmidt amplitude is constructed from first principles within a metric-stationary pseudo-Hermitian framework, providing a self-contained derivation of the finite-temperature quench dynamics. Applying this framework to an $N$-level model consisting of a two-level sector coupled to $N-2$ spectator states, we find that temperature controls the DQPTs through the redistribution of thermal weights among the eigenstates. This mechanism leads to a dimensionality-dependent threshold temperature that becomes finite when the Hilbert-space dimension reaches five, above which the Loschmidt amplitude loses all real zeros and the DQPTs are fully suppressed. The thermal suppression mechanism suggests a general principle for controlling dynamical criticality through thermal occupation, while the quasi-Hermitian framework provides the self-consistent foundation for its rigorous derivation.

</details>


### [113] [Time-independent counterdiabatic driving for emergent two-level subspaces in many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12848)
*S. Dengis,P. Schlagheck*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明量子态黎曼流形上的测地线运动可直接导出时间无关的反向绝热驱动，通过量子度量张量建立反向绝热哈密顿量与希尔伯特-施密特范数的关系，在有效二能级系统中实现恒定振幅场驱动的快速高保真度态制备，并在Landau-Zener模型、三能级STIRAP和里德伯系综中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统绝热协议需要长时间演化且依赖时变辅助控制场，寻找更高效、更简化的量子态快速制备方案是核心动机。作者试图通过黎曼流形几何方法构造时间无关的反向绝热驱动，消除对时变场形的依赖。

Method: 利用量子态黎曼流形上的测地线运动，结合量子度量张量与反向绝热哈密顿量的关系，证明恒定速度测地线使反向绝热哈密顿量的希尔伯特-施密特范数恒定；对于有效二能级系统，进一步实现反向绝热哈密顿量本身时间无关。在Landau-Zener模型、三能级STIRAP和集体驱动里德伯封锁系综中验证，并讨论多体系统中的能级泄漏限制。

Result: 时间无关的反向绝热驱动可在远短于传统绝热协议的时间尺度上实现单位保真度态制备，同时将时变辅助控制场替换为固定振幅场。但在多体系统中，有效子空间外的泄漏限制了可实现的加速程度。

Conclusion: 基于黎曼几何的反向绝热驱动方法为快速高保真度量子态制备提供了新范式，固定振幅场替代时变场简化了实验实现，但多体系统中的子空间泄漏是需要关注的关键限制因素。

Abstract: We show that geodesic motion in the Riemannian manifold of quantum states provides a direct route to time-independent counterdiabatic driving. Using the relation between the counterdiabatic Hamiltonian and the quantum metric tensor, we prove that a constant-speed geodesic makes the Hilbert-Schmidt norm of the counterdiabatic Hamiltonian constant. For effective two-level systems whose counterdiabatic correction has a fixed operator direction, this further implies that the full counterdiabatic Hamiltonian itself is time independent. We illustrate this result with the Landau-Zener model, three-level Stimulated Raman adiabatic passage and a collectively driven Rydberg ensemble in the blockade regime. Limitations of this approach in realistic many-body systems are discussed, where the two-level reduction is only emergent and leakage out of the effective subspace bounds the achievable speedup. In all cases, time-independent counterdiabatic driving achieves unit-fidelity state preparation on timescales substantially shorter than conventional adiabatic protocols while replacing temporally shaped auxiliary controls by fixed-amplitude fields.

</details>


### [114] [Unitary Synthesis with Near-Optimal T-Count for Near-Clifford Unitaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12907)
*Wang Fang,Chris Heunen,Qisheng Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种将任意n量子比特酉算子U实现为Clifford+T电路的方法，其T-count为$\widetilde{O}(2^n d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U))$，其中$d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U)$是U到Clifford群的Frobenius范数距离。当该距离为常数时T-count接近最优。该方法在$d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U) \ll 2^{n/3}$的大类酉算子上改进了Tan (2025)的$\widetilde{O}(2^{4n/3})$上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低任意酉算子的Clifford+T电路实现中的T门数量（T-count），这是量子编译中的核心开销指标，特别针对那些靠近Clifford群的酉算子。

Method: 基于酉算子U到Clifford群的Frobenius范数距离$d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U)$，设计了一种依赖该距离的合成方案，T-count与$2^n d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U)$成正比。

Result: 得到T-count上界$\widetilde{O}(2^n d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U))$，当$d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U)$为常数时接近最优；对$d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U) \ll 2^{n/3}$的算子改进了Tan (2025)的$\widetilde{O}(2^{4n/3})$上界。

Conclusion: 提出了一种依赖于酉算子与Clifford群距离的酉合成方法，在特定条件下实现了更优的T-count上界。

Abstract: We present an approach to unitary synthesis that implements an arbitrary $n$-qubit unitary operator $U$ by a Clifford+T circuit with T-count $\widetilde{O}(2^n d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U))$, where $d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U)$ is the Frobenius norm distance of $U$ to the Clifford group. The T-count is shown to be near-optimal when $d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U)$ is a constant. Our approach improves the previous best upper bound $\widetilde{O}(2^{4n/3})$ due to Tan (2025) for a large class of unitary operators $U$ as long as $d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U) \ll 2^{n/3}$.

</details>


### [115] [Heisenberg-limited metrology in the presence of non-Markovian noise with finite control rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12913)
*Shravan Shravan,Tyler G. Thurtell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了利用无限快控制恢复海森堡极限频率估计的协议与量子芝诺效应之间的直接联系，将框架扩展到无限维环境，并分析了三类协议（纯测量、测量后主动恢复、动态去耦）在有限控制频率下的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作表明，利用无限快控制（如量子纠错）可以在非马尔可夫噪声下恢复海森堡极限频率估计，但这些控制与处理马尔可夫噪声的控制有本质不同，其潜在的物理机制尚不明确。

Method: 建立了协议与量子芝诺效应的直接联系，将框架扩展至无限维环境；定义了三种协议类型（纯测量、测量后主动恢复、动态去耦），并在有限控制频率下严格分析了各自的性能。

Result: 纯测量协议在无法实施主动恢复的噪声模型下可行，但量子Fisher信息对控制频率的依赖呈二次方恶化；动态去耦协议在纯测量协议可行的条件下总是可实现的，且其量子Fisher信息对控制频率的依赖与主动恢复协议相同。

Conclusion: 动态去耦协议在有限控制频率下具有与主动恢复协议相同的标度优势，数值模拟表明其在微扰理论适用范围之外仍可能有效。

Abstract: Recently, it has been shown that protocols utilizing infinitely fast controls, such as quantum error correction, can in principle restore Heisenberg-limited frequency estimation in the presence of a broad class of non-Markovian noise models arising from coupling to finite-dimensional environments. However, these controls differ substantially from those used to address Markovian noise, and their underlying physical mechanism remains unclear. In this work, we establish a direct connection between these protocols and the quantum Zeno effect, and extend the framework to infinite-dimensional environments. We delineate three types of constructions: (a) protocols that rely solely on measurements, (b) protocols using an active recovery after measurements and (c) protocols using dynamical decoupling and rigorously analyze the performance of each when controls can be applied at only a finite rate. While protocols relying purely on measurements can be engineered for noise models where active recoveries are fundamentally impossible, they result in the quantum Fisher information exhibiting a quadratically worse dependence on the control frequency. Surprisingly, while dynamical decoupling protocols are possible whenever protocols relying only on measurements can be engineered, the quantum Fisher information has the same dependence on control frequency as the active recovery protocols. Numerical simulations suggest that the improvement offered by dynamical decoupling may work in regimes beyond the perturbative setting where our rigorous theorems apply.

</details>


### [116] [Logical Entangling with Phantom Codes in Hypergraph Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12948)
*Keming He,Ziao Tang,Zetong Li,Ge Bai,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在二元CSS超图乘积码中，只有单纯形-重复码族（至多自然等价意义下）满足幻影条件——即通过物理量子比特置换和泡利帧更新实现块内逻辑CNOT门。该码族在电路级噪声下的GHZ态制备和特罗特化多体量子模拟中优于旋转表面码基线，且可重构中性原子阵列为其提供了自然实现平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑纠缠门是容错量子计算中物理时空开销的主要来源。幻影码通过物理置换和泡利帧更新来降低这一成本，但能否与qLDPC码的低权重稳定子结构兼容是低开销容错架构的核心问题。本文旨在给出确定性回答，并寻找在HGP框架下可行的码族。

Method: 在二元CSS超图乘积码框架内，通过严格数学证明刻画满足幻影条件的码族（至多自然等价意义下唯一为单纯形-重复码族）；随后在电路级噪声下对该码族进行GHZ态制备和特罗特化量子模拟的性能评估。

Result: 1) 证明了单纯形-重复码族是HGP码中唯一（至多自然等价）满足幻影条件的家族；2) 该码族保留低权重稳定子检查；3) 在GHZ态制备和特罗特化多体量子模拟两个基准任务中，相对于旋转表面码基线展现出具体优势；4) 可重构中性原子阵列能够自然支持该方案。

Conclusion: 研究精确界定了在HGP框架下基于置换的逻辑纠缠对码设计的约束条件，展示了唯一可行码族在电路级噪声下的优势，并为在低开销中性原子容错硬件上寻找具有更好渐近参数的幻影qLDPC码族提供了指导方向。

Abstract: Logical entangling gates are a major source of physical spacetime overhead in fault-tolerant quantum computation. Phantom codes reduce this cost by implementing every ordered in-block logical CNOT through physical qubit permutations and Pauli-frame updates. Whether this mechanism can coexist with the low-weight stabilizer structure of qLDPC codes is a central question for low-overhead fault-tolerant architectures. We give a deterministic answer within binary CSS hypergraph product (HGP) codes. Up to natural equivalences, the simplex-repetition family is the unique HGP family satisfying the phantom condition. We then evaluate this family under circuit-level noise in logical GHZ-state preparation and Trotterized many-body quantum simulation. The codes retain low-weight stabilizer checks and yield concrete advantages over rotated surface-code baselines in both benchmarks. Reconfigurable neutral-atom arrays offer a natural setting for this approach, supporting nonlocal qLDPC operations while enabling in-block logical CNOTs without additional physical operations. Together, these results make precise how permutation-based logical entangling constrains code design within the HGP framework, demonstrate the circuit-level benefits of the unique family, and guide the search for phantom qLDPC families with better asymptotic parameters for low-overhead fault tolerance on neutral-atom hardware.

</details>


### [117] [Nonreciprocal Quantum Mpemba Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12966)
*Wei-Bin Yan,Ying-Jie Zhang,Yun-Jie Xia,Heng Fan,Zhong-Xiao Man*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种非互易量子Mpemba效应：在对称耦合 reservoirs 的开放量子系统中，通过交换两个 reservoirs 的参数（swap操作），可以在不改变初始态和谱结构的情况下，开关量子Mpemba效应，其机制完全由本征向量承载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子Mpemba效应中非互易性的存在及其背后的机制，特别是能否在不改变系统谱结构的前提下，通过某种操作切换该效应的开关状态。

Method: 考虑一类开放量子系统，每个系统通过对称端口耦合到两个同构的 reservoirs。引入 swap 操作（交换两 reservoirs 的参数），分析该操作对Liouvillian算符、本征值和本征向量的影响，研究其如何改变远态（far state）在慢模上的投影，从而开关量子Mpemba效应。

Result: Swap操作不改变Liouvillian的本征值（谱结构保持不变），仅旋转本征向量。这种非互易性完全由本征向量承载：swap改变了远态在慢模上的投影，切换了它是否绕过最慢弛豫通道。在Liouvillian异常点处，远态的弛豫从绕过慢模转变为避免临界慢化，且开关对比度保持完整。

Conclusion: 证实了非互易量子Mpemba效应的存在，其机制独立于谱结构，在Liouvillian异常点处达到最纯粹的形式。这一发现揭示了量子Mpemba效应中本征向量的关键作用，为量子热力学和非平衡动力学提供了新的理解维度。

Abstract: We demonstrate a nonreciprocal quantum Mpemba effect. Consider a broad class of open quantum systems, each coupled to two isomorphic reservoirs through symmetric ports. Interchanging the parameters of the two reservoirs -- a discrete operation we call the swap -- turns the quantum Mpemba effect on or off without changing the initial states. The swap modifies the Liouvillian, yet a structural symmetry pins the eigenvalues while rotating only the eigenvectors. The nonreciprocity therefore leaves no trace in the spectrum and is carried entirely by the eigenvectors. Concretely, the swap alters the far state's projection onto the slowest mode, switching whether it bypasses the slowest relaxation channel. At a Liouvillian exceptional point, the far state's relaxation switches from bypassing the slowest mode to avoiding the critical slowing, with the on--off contrast intact. There the spectrum-independent mechanism takes its purest form.

</details>


### [118] [An Agentic Formalization for Certified Quantum Neural Network Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12981)
*Mingrui Jing,Lei Zhang,Yusheng Zhao,Hongshun Yao,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Lean 4证明核中形式化了量子神经网络(QNN)的表述性与可训练性理论，给出了单量子比特QNN的充要刻画、量子相位处理定理、过参数化上界、去圆化二阶矩接口下的直接和损失方差定律，以及DLA坐标下的条件方差定律与精确损失重构的顶石定理，并发现了八处非正式论证中未明确的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子神经网络的設計需要在表述性(expressivity)和可训练性(trainability)之间取得平衡，现有理论分别通过电路函数分析(如量子信号处理)和动力学李代数(DLA)方法进行研究，但缺乏统一的、机器可验证的形式化基础。

Method: 在Lean 4证明核中构建连接的形式化开发，每个分析输入要么被证明要么作为命名假设暴露；单量子比特QNN的充要刻画、有资源计数的量子相位处理定理、基于DLA维度的量子Fisher信息秩上界、去圆化二阶矩接口下的直接和损失方差定律、参数化的Casimir唯一性引擎、单量子比特和乘积Clifford系综的两设计假设闭合，以及DLA坐标下的条件方差定律与精确损失重构。

Result: 实现了QNN表述性与可训练性主要组件的机器可验证形式化，发现了八处非正式论证中未明确指出的修正与澄清。

Conclusion: 该工作为QNN理论提供了机器可验证的基础，为AI辅助或自动化的量子机器学习算法设计迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: A central model in quantum machine learning is the quantum neural network (QNN), whose design requires balancing expressivity and trainability. Technically, expressivity is studied through circuit-function analysis, such as quantum signal processing, while trainability is analyzed using dynamical-Lie-algebra (DLA) methods. To support certified QNN design, we formalize these major components of QNN theory in a connected lean 4 development checked by a proof kernel, where every analytic input is either proved or exposed as a named hypothesis. On the expressivity side, we prove exact if-and-only-if characterizations of single-qubit QNNs, a resource-counted quantum phase processing theorem, and an overparameterization ceiling that bounds the quantum Fisher information rank by the DLA dimension. On the trainability side, we derive the direct-sum loss-variance law through a de-circularized second-moment interface. A parameterized Casimir-uniqueness engine discharges the required inputs for fully controllable, orthogonal, and matchgate circuit families, while single-qubit and product-Clifford ensembles close the two-design assumptions directly. A capstone theorem pairs the conditional variance law with exact loss reconstruction in DLA coordinates. The development record identifies eight corrections and clarifications that were not explicit in the informal arguments. We expect this work to provide a machine-checkable foundation for QNN theory and a step toward AI-assisted or automated design of quantum machine learning algorithms.

</details>


### [119] [A Noise-Aware Quantum Algorithm for Credit Valuation Adjustments on Real Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12990)
*Guillem Borràs Espert,Francisco Gómez Casanova,Luis de Pedro Sánchez,Senaida Hernández Santana,Pablo Serrano Molinero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个端到端的噪声感知量子工作流，用于信用估值调整（CVA）计算。该工作流结合了基于QCBM的联合时间-市场分布编码、受控支付旋转以及一种新颖的对比感知贝叶斯迭代量子振幅估计（CABIQAE）方法。硬件校准实验表明，CABIQAE在利用当前量子设备的有限放大能力方面优于噪声无关的替代方案，且经典后处理运行时间远低于噪声感知的BAE基线。研究还将CVA总误差分解为统计、编码、离散化和硬件贡献，并指出完整的CVA量子线路仍受限于电路深度和离散化分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用估值调整（CVA）需要重复进行风险中性期望估计，是量子振幅估计的自然测试平台。虽然量子振幅估计的相干放大原则上可以降低蒙特卡洛采样成本，但这一优势能否在真实的金融编码和噪声硬件条件下保持尚不清楚。

Method: 开发了端到端的噪声感知量子工作流，包括市场校准、离散化、量子线路构建、硬件执行和误差预算分析。模型结合了相关双资产暴露与贴现和违约因子，通过QCBM（量子电路玻恩机）编码联合时间-市场分布，并采用受控支付旋转。提出了对比感知贝叶斯迭代量子振幅估计（CABIQAE），将实验校准的Grover对比度损失纳入贝叶斯推理和电路深度选择中。

Result: 硬件校准实验表明，CABIQAE在当前量子设备上比噪声无关的替代方案更有效地利用了有限的放大能力，且经典后处理运行时间远低于噪声感知的BAE基线。CVA总误差被分解为统计、编码、离散化和硬件四个贡献部分。

Conclusion: 完整的CVA量子线路仍受限于电路深度和离散化分辨率，表明当前量子硬件在金融应用中仍面临可扩展性挑战。

Abstract: Credit Valuation Adjustment (CVA) requires repeated risk-neutral expectation estimation, making it a natural test bed for quantum amplitude estimation, whose coherent amplification can in principle reduce Monte Carlo sampling cost. Whether this advantage survives realistic financial encoding and noisy hardware remains open. We develop an end-to-end, noise-aware quantum workflow for CVA, covering market calibration, discretisation, oracle construction, hardware execution and error-budget analysis. The model combines a correlated two-asset exposure with discount and default factors, encoded through a QCBM-based joint time-market distribution and controlled payoff rotations. We introduce contrast-aware Bayesian iterative quantum amplitude estimation (CABIQAE), which incorporates experimentally calibrated Grover-contrast loss into Bayesian inference and circuit-depth selection. Hardware-calibrated experiments show that CABIQAE exploits the limited amplification available on current devices more effectively than noise-agnostic alternatives and achieves a much lower classical post-processing runtime than the noise-aware BAE baseline. The analysis further decomposes the total CVA error into statistical, encoding, discretisation and hardware contributions. The full CVA oracle remains limited by circuit depth and discretisation resolution.

</details>


### [120] [Expressibility and trainability of a two-dimensional pairwise quantum-circuit ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12996)
*Shuai Zhang,Wei Liu,Ji-Chong Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种原生2D参数化量子电路（PQC）架构，模拟超导量子比特的平面连接拓扑，并在16量子比特系统上与三种1D电路（链/环结构）进行对比。实验表明，在浅层（L=1-2）时2D电路的KL散度最小，二阶帧势更接近理论下限；在固定参数下，2D电路的梯度方差在L=1-4时更小，但随层数增加差异逐渐消失（L=6时四者统计一致）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PQC设计多采用硬件无关或简化的1D链/环纠缠模式，但量子硬件（如超导量子比特）的拓扑结构正趋向于原生2D平面连接。为了充分利用硬件拓扑优势，需要设计匹配的2D电路结构，并系统评估其表达能力与可训练性。

Method: 构建原生2D配对ansatz（匹配超导量子比特平面拓扑），与三种1D ansatz在相同层深下对比。使用KL散度衡量概率分布差异，用二阶帧势评估表达能力，通过Pauli-Z-string期望值的梯度方差评估可训练性。固定16量子比特系统，在不同层数L下进行实验。

Result: 在L=1-2时，2D ansatz的KL散度最小；浅层时2D电路的二阶帧势更快逼近理论下限。对于梯度方差，L=1-4时2D电路更小，L=5时差异缩小，L=6时四种ansatz的方差在统计上一致。

Conclusion: 原生2D PQC在浅层时具有更优的表达能力和更小的梯度方差，但随着电路深度增加，其优势逐渐减弱至与1D电路相当。这为设计匹配硬件拓扑的量子电路提供了实验依据，提示浅层场景下2D设计更有利。

Abstract: Parameterized quantum circuits~(PQCs) constitute a central building block of variational quantum algorithms~(VQAs) and quantum machine learning~(QML) methods. Existing ansatz designs often adopt hardware-agnostic or simplified 1D chain/ring entanglement patterns. However, as quantum hardware continues to develop, native 2D connectivity patterns, such as planar superconducting-qubit architectures, are becoming increasingly important. Inspired by this hardware structure, we construct a native 2D pairwise ansatz and compare its expressibility and trainability with representative 1D ansatze at identical layer depths, despite their different circuit depths. For the fixed 16-qubit system, the 2D ansatz has the smallest KL divergence at $L=1$ and $2$, and its second-order frame potential approaches the theoretical lower bound more rapidly at shallow layer counts than the frame potentials of the three 1D ansatze. We also evaluate the gradient variance of the Pauli-$Z$-string expectation value $\langle Z_0\otimes\cdots\otimes Z_{15}\rangle$ with respect to the first $R_y$ angle. For this Pauli-$Z$ string and fixed parameter, the gradient variance is smaller for the 2D circuit at $L=1$--$4$. The differences narrow at $L=5$, and the four ansatze yield statistically compatible variances at $L=6$.

</details>


### [121] [Spin Chain Quantum Communication on a Trapped-Ion Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12999)
*Madhumita Sarkar,Trinity Pointon,Sougato Bose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在IonQ的 trapped-ion 量子处理器上，通过数字模拟自旋哈密顿量，实验实现了工程化的量子通信协议。研究表明，工程化耦合分布能显著提升量子态传输保真度，且利用哈密顿量对易结构实现的并行Trotter分解在降低电路深度的同时更精确地再现了目标动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现远距离量子比特间的高效通信是扩展量子处理器的核心挑战之一。尽管理论上已对工程化自旋链协议进行了广泛研究，但其实验实现仍然有限。

Method: 在IonQ的 trapped-ion 量子处理器上数字模拟自旋哈密顿量，结合精确数值模拟与量子硬件实验，比较均匀最近邻耦合与工程化耦合分布的性能，并利用哈密顿量对易结构实现并行Trotter分解。

Result: 工程化相互作用显著提升了量子态传输的保真度；并行Trotter分解相比传统顺序实现大幅降低了电路深度和执行时间，同时更忠实地再现了目标动力学。

Conclusion: 可编程量子处理器能够有效实现并高效执行量子通信协议，推动了基于哈密顿量的量子通信向实用量子技术迈进。

Abstract: Efficient communication between distant qubits is one of the central challenges in scaling quantum processors. Although engineered spin chain protocols have been extensively investigated theoretically, their experimental realization has remained comparatively limited. Here, we experimentally realize engineered quantum communication protocols through digitally simulated spin Hamiltonian on IonQ's Forte 1/ Forte Enterprise 1 trapped-ion quantum processor. Combining exact numerical simulations with quantum hardware experiments, we benchmark uniform nearest-neighbour and engineered coupling profiles and demonstrate that engineered interactions significantly enhance the fidelity of quantum state transfer. We further show that exploiting the commutation structure of the spin Hamiltonian enables a parallel Trotter decomposition that more faithfully reproduces the target dynamics while substantially reducing the circuit depth and execution time compared to the conventional sequential implementations. Our results demonstrate that programmable quantum processors can effectively realize and efficiently implement quantum communication protocols, bringing Hamiltonian-based quantum communication closer to practical quantum technologies.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Noncommutative Floquet--Bloch Theory for Nilpotent Groups:\ Representation-Theoretic Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12069)
*Atsushi Katsuda*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文针对非交换幂零格子的Floquet-Bloch理论，利用有理Kirillov数据和有限维有理纤维构造了精确的Bloch型替代，并证明了限制定理、傅里叶逆变换和Plancherel测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Floquet-Bloch理论适用于交换周期问题，但对于非交换幂零格子，由于非I型障碍，无法用类似方式参数化整个酉对偶。本文旨在绕过这一障碍，在表示论部分构建精确的Bloch型替代。

Method: 利用有理Kirillov参数和有理极化构造诱导表示来描述分支律；在有限维有理纤维上构造正有限可加Plancherel测度，建立傅里叶逆变换和归一化迹恒等式。

Result: 对无挠有限生成幂零群Γ及其Malcev完备化G，证明了与有理Kirillov参数相伴的不可约酉表示π_l在Γ上的精确限制定理；在有限维有理纤维上构造了Plancherel测度，恢复并推广了离散Heisenberg情形下的Pytlik公式。

Conclusion: 本文为非交换幂零周期性结构提供了严格的调和分析基础，所构造的Bloch型分解和Plancherel理论可应用于更广泛的非交换周期问题。

Abstract: Classical Floquet--Bloch theory decomposes abelian periodic problems over the character torus of the lattice. For nonabelian nilpotent lattices, the non--type~I obstruction rules out a comparable parametrization of the full unitary dual. We do not attempt to remove this obstruction. Instead, we construct an exact Bloch-type replacement on the representation-theoretic part of the theory which is visible from rational Kirillov data and from finite-dimensional rational fibers.
  Let \(Γ\) be a torsion-free finitely generated nilpotent group and let \(G\) be its Malcev completion. For an irreducible unitary representation \(π_l\) of \(G\) attached to a rational Kirillov parameter \(l\in\mathfrak g_{\mathbb Q}^{*}\), we prove an exact restriction theorem for \(π_l|_Γ\). The branching is first described by induced representations attached to rational polarizations. On the rational odd locus relevant to finite-dimensional representations, it further decomposes into finite-dimensional irreducible representations of \(Γ\).
  On these finite-dimensional rational fibers we construct a positive finitely additive Plancherel measure. It gives Fourier inversion and normalized trace identities for nilpotent lattices, recovering Pytlik's formula in the discrete Heisenberg case.

</details>


### [123] [Stable Limit DAHA of type $(C^{\vee},C)$ and Stable Limit Koornwinder Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12276)
*Dongyu Wu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了$(C^\vee,C)$型双仿射Hecke代数的正、负稳定极限表示，作用于几乎对称Laurent多项式空间，并通过极限Cherednik算子的三角作用与部分对称化算子得到了两组极限Koornwinder多项式基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限秩$(C_n^\vee,C_n)$型DAHA的标准多项式表示出发，研究参数$t$的正负幂次重新标度下Cherednik算子的渐近行为，以构造无限秩极限下的稳定表示。

Method: 1) 对Cherednik算子进行正幂次和负幂次两种重新标度；2) 证明标度后的算子在几乎对称Laurent多项式环上存在良定义极限；3) 在由元组-分区符号标记的自然基上证明极限算子的三角性（相对于诱导Bruhat序）；4) 利用部分对称化算子作用于非对称Koornwinder多项式，构造极限Cherednik算子的同时本征函数系。

Result: 得到了正、负两种稳定极限DAHA表示；两组极限Cherednik算子的本征函数（正/负稳定极限Koornwinder多项式）各自构成几乎对称Laurent多项式空间的基底。

Conclusion: 成功建立了$(C^\vee,C)$型DAHA在无限秩极限下的两种稳定表示理论，并构造了相应的正交多项式基，推广了Koornwinder多项式到稳定极限情形。

Abstract: We construct two stable limit representations of the double affine Hecke algebra of type $(C^\vee,C)$ on the space of almost symmetric Laurent polynomials, namely the positive and negative stable limit representations. Starting from the standard polynomial representation of the finite rank DAHA of type $(C_n^\vee,C_n)$, we study the asymptotic behavior of the Cherednik operators under the two natural rescalings by positive and negative powers of the parameter $t$. We prove that these rescaled Cherednik operators admit well-defined limits on the ring of almost symmetric Laurent polynomials. This yields stable positive and negative actions of a common stable limit DAHA. The action of the limit Cherednik operators is also proven to be triangular on a natural basis of almost symmetric Laurent polynomials labeled by tuple-partition symbols with respect to the induced Bruhat order. We further construct for each of the two stable limit representations a set of simultaneous eigenfunctions of the limit Cherednik operators using the partial symmetrization operators acting on the non-symmetric Koornwinder polynomials. We show that each of the two sets of the eigenfunctions form a basis of the space of almost symmetric Laurent polynomials, and denote them by the positive and negative stable limit Koornwinder polynomials.

</details>


### [124] [Homomorphisms between standard modules of generalized Reedy categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12499)
*Liping Li*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种表示论方法研究广义Reedy范畴，通过标准模之间的同态空间将抽象同调构造简化为线性代数和谱图论问题，并统一了Dold-Kan对应、Kuhn分解定理和Thévenaz-Webb半单性定理等经典结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为广义Reedy范畴建立一个统一的、可计算的表示论框架，将抽象的范畴同调构造转化为初等的线性代数与谱图论问题，并统一解释代数拓扑与表示论中多个看似无关的经典定理。

Method: 采用表示论方法，系统研究广义Reedy范畴中标准模之间的同态空间；通过关联矩阵和态射纤维将同调构造还原为线性代数和谱图论计算。

Result: 建立了统一的、可计算的框架，将抽象同调构造简化为线性代数与谱图论；统一推广了Dold-Kan对应至根树范畴（包括有限链、有限集与部分单射、有限蜘蛛等）；统一了Kuhn分解定理和Thévenaz-Webb半单性定理。

Conclusion: 该工作为广义Reedy范畴提供了统一的表示论视角，将多个经典结果纳入同一概念基础，展示了范畴表示论在同调代数中的强大统一能力。

Abstract: We develop a representation-theoretic approach to generalized Reedy categories through a systematic study of homomorphism spaces between standard modules. For a broad class of these categories, we provide a uniform, computable framework that reduces abstract homological constructions to elementary linear algebra and spectral graph theory via incidence matrices and morphism fibers. As a primary application, we establish a uniform extension of the Dold--Kan correspondence for categories arising from rooted trees, encompassing the categories of finite chains, finite sets and partial injections, and finite spiders. Crucially, this machinery unifies and provides a singular conceptual basis for several classic, seemingly disparate results across algebraic topology and representation theory, including Kuhn's decomposition theorem for vector spaces and the Thévenaz--Webb semisimplicity theorem for Mackey functors.

</details>


### [125] [Möbius functions for pseudo-Levi subgroups in finite general linear and symplectic groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12522)
*Aritra Kundu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文计算了包含固定极大环面的伪Levi子群集合上的Möbius函数，针对有限群族GL_n(q)和Sp_{2n}(q)，该函数对张量积特征标分解公式的显式求值至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪Levi子群集合上的Möbius函数是显式计算有限约化群张量积特征标分解公式的关键，该公式由论文[arXiv:2512.01432]的Theorem 2给出。

Method: 对有限群族GL_n(q)和Sp_{2n}(q)中，包含固定极大环面的伪Levi子群构成的偏序集，计算其Möbius函数。

Result: 得到了GL_n(q)和Sp_{2n}(q)中伪Levi子群偏序集上的Möbius函数的显式表达式。

Conclusion: 本文的结果为显式计算论文[arXiv:2512.01432]中张量积特征标分解公式提供了必要的组合工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we compute the Möbius function on the set of pseudo-Levi subgroups containing a fixed maximal torus of two families of finite groups, $GL_n(q)$ and $Sp_{2n}(q)$, for some natural number $n$ and a prime power $q$. The Möbius function for the set of pseudo-Levi subgroups of a finite group $G$ is important for an explicit evaluation of the formula expressing the decomposition of tensor products of characters, as proved in Theorem 2 of the paper ''Multiplicity of characters of finite reductive groups and Drinfeld doubles'' [arXiv:2512.01432].

</details>


### [126] [A universal leading-residue formula for Witten zeta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12728)
*Jonas Matuzas*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Au的归一化Witten zeta函数在s=2/h处有单极点，并给出了留数表达式，从而确立了其伽玛乘积形式的猜想。证明了适当抛物型分层是严格次临界的，并获得了每个简单类型下维数不超过X的不可约表示数量的直接渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在证明Au关于归一化Witten zeta函数的极点位置及留数的猜想，以及其伽玛乘积形式的猜想，并给出不可约表示计数的显式渐近常数。

Method: 证明将主导格系数与一个收敛的球面Coxeter判别式积分在临界指数处联系起来，并利用Macdonald-Mehta-Opdam恒等式的边界极点来评估该积分。同时证明适当抛物型分层是严格次临界的。

Result: 证明了Au的归一化Witten zeta函数在s=2/h处有单极点，留数由显式公式给出，包含了Cartan矩阵行列式、Weyl群阶、Coxeter数以及伽玛函数乘积。验证了Au在A4类型中的预测。获得了每个简单类型下维数不超过X的不可约表示数量的直接非Tauber型渐近公式。

Conclusion: 该工作完整证明了Au关于Witten zeta函数的猜想，建立了根系统理论中zeta函数渐近行为的精确刻画，并为不可约表示计数提供了显式常数。

Abstract: Let $Φ$ be an irreducible crystallographic root system of rank $r$, with Coxeter number $h$, Weyl group $W$, Cartan matrix $C_Φ$, and invariant degrees $2=d_1\leq\cdots\leq d_r=h$. We prove that Au's normalized Witten zeta function $ξ_Φ(s)$ has a simple pole at $s=2/h$, with residue $\mathop{\rm Res}_{s=2/h}ξ_Φ(s)=\frac{2(2π)^{r/2}\sqrt{\det C_Φ}}{h|W|}\frac{\prod_{i=1}^{r-1}Γ(1-d_i/h)}{Γ(1-1/h)^r}$. The proof identifies the leading lattice coefficient with a convergent spherical Coxeter-discriminant integral at the critical exponent and evaluates this integral using the boundary pole of the Macdonald--Mehta--Opdam identity. Proper parabolic strata are shown to be strictly subcritical. This establishes Au's gamma-product-shape conjecture and his prediction in type $A_4$. We also obtain a direct, non-Tauberian asymptotic, with an explicit constant for every simple type, for the number of irreducible representations of dimension at most $X$.

</details>

### [127] [Strong-to-Weak Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking from Wormholes in Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12022)
*Taishi Kawamoto,Kenya Tasuki,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-th

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索混合态中特有的强到弱自发对称性破缺（SWSSB）这一新型临界现象在有限温度下的行为，并理解其在全息对偶（特别是AdS/CFT）中的实现机制。

Method: 以BTZ黑洞作为全息模型，利用热场双重量构中的虫洞几何来展示SWSSB的实现；通过系综平均将SWSSB重新表述为全局对称性的涌现；结合沼泽地计划进行分析并提出新的猜想。

Result: 证明了SWSSB可通过BTZ黑洞的虫洞几何实现；揭示了SWSSB与系综平均下全局对称性涌现之间的等价关系；提出了一个关于涉及系综平均的量子引力理论的新沼泽地猜想。

Conclusion: SWSSB在全息框架下具有清晰的几何实现（虫洞），且与系综平均的对称性涌现紧密相关；这一发现可能对量子引力中沼泽地条件的理解提供新的视角和约束。

Abstract: We study strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB) at finite temperature and its implications in holography. SWSSB is a novel critical phenomenon that arises only in mixed states. We demonstrate SWSSB with the BTZ black hole and show that, in the bulk, it is realized through wormhole geometries connecting the two copies in the thermofield-double construction. We point out that SWSSB can be recast as an emergence of global symmetries under ensemble averaging. We further examine the interplay between SWSSB and the swampland program, and propose a new swampland conjecture for quantum-gravity theories involving ensemble averages.

</details>


### [128] [Group Theory and the CFT Distance Conjecture: $\mathcal{N}=2$ Tensionless Strings Have No (Co)Weight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12014)
*Florent Baume,Fabio Mantegazza*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了四维大N N=2超共形场论在无限距离点处的Hagedorn行为，发现Hagedorn温度由仿射或有限Cartan邻接矩阵的最大特征值决定，定义了两种普适类：仿射情形（所有轨道/定向影射共享相同温度）和ADE分类情形（温度由对偶Coxeter数决定）。结果适用于所有经典规范对称性的大N N=2 quiver理论，并讨论了弦论构造、全息含义及对低超对称理论的推广。研究还给出了CFT距离猜想预言的指数速率上下界，当quiver只有一个规范节点时下界被饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解四维大N N=2 SCFT在共形流形无限距离点处的高能态密度指数增长行为（Hagedorn行为），探究其与弦谱的相似性，并检验CFT距离猜想。

Method: 系统调查具有拉格朗日描述的四维大N N=2 SCFT，利用Lie代数编码quiver形状，通过仿射或有限Cartan邻接矩阵的最大特征值确定Hagedorn温度，分析不同普适类，并推广到低超对称理论。

Result: 定义了两种Hagedorn行为普适类：仿射类（所有轨道/定向影射N=4 SYM投影共享相同温度）和ADE类（温度由对偶Coxeter数决定）。给出了CFT距离猜想预言的指数速率上下界，单规范节点时下界饱和，自然解释了文献中发现的三种普适类。

Conclusion: 大N N=2 quiver理论的Hagedorn温度由Cartan邻接矩阵最大特征值统一描述，仿射类对应弦谱特征，ADE类对应更丰富的结构。结果支持CFT距离猜想，并为理解高能态密度与距离猜想的关系提供了框架。

Abstract: We perform a systematic survey of the Hagedorn behaviour at infinite-distance points in the conformal manifold of four-dimensional large-$N$ $\mathcal{N}=2$ Superconformal Field Theories admitting a Lagrangian description. Many properties of these theories can be understood in terms of the Lie algebra encoding the shape of their quiver. We find that in the overall-free limit, the Hagedorn temperature is determined by the largest eigenvalue of an affine or finite Cartan adjacency matrix. This defines two types of universality classes of theories sharing the same high-energy exponential growth of states characteristic of string-like spectra. The first and largest is the affine case, corresponding to orbifold and orientifold projections of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super-Yang-Mills, and all share the same temperature. The others fall into universality classes following an ADE classification with a temperature set by the dual Coxeter number, and can be obtained by deforming the affine case. Our results apply to all large-$N$ $\mathcal{N}=2$ quivers with any classical gauge symmetry, including those with matter charged beyond bifundamental representations. We further discuss the string-theoretic construction of these theories and some of the holographic implications, as well as how our methods extend to broad families of theories with less supersymmetry. We also consider limits where only part of the theory becomes free, and find lower and upper bounds on the exponential rate predicted by the CFT Distance Conjecture. Both these bounds and the Hagedorn temperature are set by the same eigenvalue, and when the quiver has a single gauge node the lower bound is saturated, giving a natural explanation for the three universality classes recently found in the literature.

</details>


### [129] [Low-Temperature Holographic Conductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12064)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究极低温度下全息电导率的量子涨落效应，特别是在近极端AdS₄黑洞喉部区域强耦合量子涨落对电导率的影响机制。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：(1) 有效二维作用量（Schwarzian模式）描述CT尺度上的量子涨落；(2) 直接从四维引力路径积分计算一圈量子修正。

Result: 量子修正电导率呈现非单调温度依赖：随温度降低先下降，在CT_min≈0.023处达到最小值，随后在CT≪1时以1/√CT形式增长。两种方法在CT≫1区域定性一致。

Conclusion: 量子修正从根本上改变了低温全息电导率的行为，两种不同的理论方法给出了定性一致的结果。

Abstract: We revisit holographic electrical conductivity in the regime of very low temperatures where quantum fluctuations in the throat of the near-extremal, asymptotically AdS$_4$ dual black brane are strongly coupled. We treat the fluctuations via an effective two-dimensional action capturing the effects of the Schwarzian modes at the scale $1/C$. Our main result is a non-monotonic temperature dependence: the quantum-corrected conductivity decreases as the temperature is lowered, reaches a minimum at $(CT_{\mathrm{min}}\simeq 0.023)$, and grows as $(1/\sqrt{CT})$ for $(CT\ll 1)$. We further estimate the one-loop contribution to the conductivity directly from the four-dimensional gravitational path integral and find qualitative agreement with the effective two-dimensional description in the regime $(CT\gg 1)$. We find that quantum corrections substantially modify the low-temperature behavior of the holographic conductivity in both approaches.

</details>


### [130] [The Hitchhiker's Guide to Tensionless String Limits in 4d SCFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12019)
*José Calderón-Infante,Angel M. Uranga,Irene Valenzuela*

TL;DR: 本文研究4d ${\cal N}=4,2,1$超共形规范理论在弱耦合无限距离极限下的普适类，利用Hanany-Witten构造建立其与NS5-膜系统及AdS对偶中涌现弦的关系，发现三种普适类由双标度极限中共线的NS5-膜数量决定，并推广到多规范群理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示4d超共形规范理论在弱耦合无限距离极限下普适类的起源，理解其与AdS对偶中涌现弦类型的对应关系，并从CFT视角为arxiv:2410.07309中发现的三种普适类提供统一解释。

Method: 利用Hanany-Witten构造对全体单规范群超共形理论进行显式构建，分析双标度极限下NS5-膜共线行为，通过orbifold/orientifold投影建立普适类与母理论的关系，研究世界sheet理论及D-膜反作用效应。

Result: 建立了三种普适类与NS5-膜数量的对应关系；发现同一类内的理论均来自同一母理论的orbifold/orientifold投影（含少量额外味）；确定了三种涌现弦分别对应10d IIB型弦、亚临界弦及一种新型弦；将结果推广到多规范群及准共形规范理论。

Conclusion: 从CFT和全息对偶角度完整解释了4d超共形理论弱耦合极限下普适类的起源及涌现弦类型，所发展的工具可广泛应用于更一般理论，揭示了弦论框架下SCFT网络的深层结构。

Abstract: We study the appearance of universality classes in overall weak-coupling infinite-distance limits in the conformal manifold of 4d ${\cal N}=4,2,1$ superconformal gauge theories at large $N$ and their relation to the type of weakly-coupled emergent string in the AdS bulk dual using brane models. We focus on the complete set of theories with simple gauge group, and provide explicit Hanany-Witten constructions for each of them, including those that have non-Einstein bulk duals. This establishes that the three universality classes found in arXiv:2410.07309 are determined by the number of NS5-branes becoming coincident in a suitable double-scaling limit required to obtain the SCFT. Furthermore, all theories within a given universality class descend from the same parent theory by performing orbifold/orientifold projections (with possibly a small number of extra flavors), which are subleading effects at large $N$. This explains the origin of the universality classes from a CFT perspective. Moreover, we argue that theories in the same universality class share the same closed-string background -- generated by the double-scaled NS5-brane system -- in which the backreaction of D-branes is expected to generate the AdS throat holographically dual to the SCFT. We describe the corresponding worldsheet theories, and find that the three kinds of emergent tensionless strings correspond to the 10d type IIB string, a subcritical string previously studied in a related context, and a novel kind of string which we characterize in detail. Our tools are not limited to this set of SCFTs, but can be naturally applied to more general theories, and allow us to study interpolating models yielding a network of SCFTs related by partial weak-coupling limits. We explicitly discuss the generalization of our results to large classes of theories with more than one gauge factor and even to some quasi-conformal gauge theories.

</details>

Motivation: 探究近极端黑洞近AdS₂喉区涨落如何影响全息模型中动量弛豫系统的输运性质，寻找量子引力在凝聚态系统中的可观测特征。
