Abstract: We determine the quantum corrections to transport arising from fluctuations of the near-AdS${}_2$ throat of near-extremal black branes in holographic models with momentum relaxation. By computing the shear viscosity and electrical conductivity at both zero and finite chemical potential, we uncover a universal low-temperature enhancement of transport generated by Schwarzian quantum fluctuations. Specifically, transport coefficients extracted from retarded Green's functions increase throughout the regime $C T \ll 1$. For operators with Schwarzian scaling dimension $Δ>1$, this enhancement is preceded by a universal minimum at a characteristic temperature $T_{\rm min} \propto C^{-1}$, leading to a non-monotonic temperature dependence strikingly similar to that observed in many correlated materials. In contrast, for the case $Δ=1$, relevant for the electrical conductivity, the transport coefficient evolves monotonically toward a constant value. Our results identify universal signatures of near-horizon quantum gravity in the transport properties of holographic quantum matter.

</details>


### [132] [Strongly-coupled black holes (are not) at weak coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12020)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为弱引力猜想提供更严格的数学证明，并排除先前证明[arXiv:2401.14449]中可能存在的逻辑漏洞。

Method: 通过数学分析推导球对称带电黑洞视界外模空间游走的上界，考虑电荷Q(φ)与渐近值的关系，并针对特定方向（Q(φ)非递减）给出相同界限。

Result: 证明了在Q(φ)不小于渐近值的区域中，模空间总游走小于π/(2√k_N)；在Q(φ)非递减的方向上同样满足该界限。

Conclusion: 该界限成功排除了微扰玻色弦理论中弱引力猜想证明的一个潜在漏洞，加强了该猜想的理论基础。

Abstract: We prove sharp bounds on the moduli space excursion that is possible outside the event horizon of a spherically symmetric charged black hole. In regions where the black hole charge $Q (φ)$ is no less than its asymptotic value, we prove that the total moduli space excursion is less than $\fracπ{2\sqrt{k_N}}$ where $k_N$ is the rationalized Newton constant. We also show that the moduli space excursion in a specified direction in which $Q (φ)$ is non-decreasing satisfies the same bound. Applying our bound to electrically charged black holes, we rule out a potential loophole in a recent proof [arXiv:2401.14449] of the Weak Gravity Conjecture in perturbative bosonic string theory.

</details>

Result: 发现IIA和IIB型弦论的单圈有效作用量中存在膨胀子乘以十维欧拉密度的项，这些项源自世界面超共形鬼场的零模，看似违反软膨胀子定理。

Abstract: We show that the one loop effective action of type IIA and IIB string theories in ten dimensions have terms proportional to the dilaton times the ten dimensional Euler density, in apparent violation of the soft dilaton theorem. This can be traced to the existence of the zero modes of the world-sheet superconformal ghost fields and resolves a puzzle that arose recently in the analysis of black hole index when we compactify these theories on Calabi-Yau manifolds of non-zero Euler number.

</details>


### [134] [The Infraparticle Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13001)
*Soo-Jong Rey*

TL;DR: 本文从软QED量子仪器出发，推导了带电粒子谱边缘。通过两次投影（迹除未分辨光子和推向总能量）得到约化硬信道和包含能量分布。Laplace指数由对角软强度固定，得到ρ_inc(s)~Cθ(s-m²)(s-m²)^{-1+η_h}。保留相干核，从软环境的两次收缩推导出硬信道退相和谱边缘。对角系数κ_aa固定端点指数，而½(κ_aa+κ_bb-2Re κ_ba)固定硬选择之间的退相指数。进一步分类红外能量边际，推导有限分辨率残差Z(μ)=(μ/Λ)^{η_h}，证明红外可积扰动下的稳定性，并将浴指数与硬阈值指数分离。对于单电子谱测度，硬阈值因子是正则的，得到的边缘具有带隙非粒子谱的局部幂律，其指数仍然是未分辨光子扇区的响应系数。

Motivation: 从软QED量子仪器出发，系统推导带电粒子谱边缘的精确形式，并阐明谱边缘指数与软环境响应系数之间的内在关系。

Method: 利用软QED量子仪器的两次投影：迹除未分辨光子得到约化硬信道，推向总能量得到包含能量分布。通过软环境的两次收缩分别得到谱边缘指数和退相指数，并分类红外能量边际、推导有限分辨率残差、证明稳定性。

Result: 得到ρ_inc(s)~Cθ(s-m²)(s-m²)^{-1+η_h}；谱边缘指数由κ_aa固定，退相指数由½(κ_aa+κ_bb-2Re κ_ba)固定；有限分辨率残差Z(μ)=(μ/Λ)^{η_h}；证明了红外可积扰动下的稳定性；单电子谱测度的硬阈值因子是正则的。

Abstract: I derive the charged-particle spectral edge from the quantum instrument of soft QED. I use two projections of that instrument. Tracing over unresolved photons gives the reduced hard-sector channel. Pushing the outcomes to total energy gives the inclusive energy distribution. Its Laplace exponent is fixed by the diagonal soft intensity. For ${\rm d} N_h(ω)=η_h{\rm d}ω/ω+{\rm d} N_{h,\mathrm{reg}}(ω)$, I obtain $ρ_{\mathrm{inc}}(s)\sim Cθ(s-m^2)(s-m^2)^{-1+η_h}$. I retain the coherence kernel and derive hard-sector dephasing and the spectral edge from two contractions of one soft environment. The diagonal coefficient $κ_{aa}$ fixes the endpoint exponent, while $\frac12(κ_{aa}+κ_{bb}-2\operatorname{Re}κ_{ba})$ fixes the dephasing exponent between hard alternatives. I then classify infrared energy marginals, derive the finite-resolution residue $Z(μ)=(μ/Λ)^{η_h}$, prove stability under infrared-integrable perturbations, and separate the bath exponent from a hard threshold exponent. For the one-electron spectral measure, the hard threshold factor is regular. The resulting edge has the local power law of a gapped unparticle spectrum, while its exponent remains a response coefficient of the unresolved photon sector.

</details>


### [135] [Composite worldline instantons and the nonperturbative particle decay in constant external electric and magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12083)
*Alexander Gorsky,Ivan Poluboyarinov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论了复合世界线瞬子方法在恒定电磁场中带电粒子非微扰衰变中的有效性，证明了在不同极限下瞬子结果与自能虚部和波函数重叠方法的一致性，并探讨了质子衰变速率的估算及末态粒子纠缠效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证复合世界线瞬子方法在恒定电磁场中带电粒子非微扰衰变问题中的有效性，并建立其与自能虚部方法和波函数重叠方法之间的联系。

Method: 采用复合世界线瞬子方法计算带电粒子在恒定电场和磁场中的非微扰衰变，并在不同极限下与自能虚部方法和波函数重叠方法的结果进行对比验证。

Result: 在不同极限下，复合世界线瞬子方法得到的指数近似结果与自能虚部方法及波函数重叠方法的结果一致。

Conclusion: 复合世界线瞬子方法可用于估算外电磁场中质子衰变率（指数近似下），同时末态粒子的纠缠效应值得进一步关注。

Abstract: In this paper, we discuss the validity of the composite worldline instanton approach for the nonperturbative decay of charged particles in constant electric and magnetic fields. It is shown that the instanton results in the leading exponential approximation in the different limits agree with the ones obtained by the imaginary part of the self-energy in the external field and by overlap of the wave functions. We comment on the possible application of our results for the estimation of the decay rate of the proton in the external electric and magnetic field in the leading exponential approximation. The effects of entanglement of the particles in the final state are briefly mentioned.

</details>


### [136] [Computational homological methods for integrable field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12142)
*Marco Benini,Ryan A. Cullinan,Alexander Schenkel,Benoit Vicedo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为从4维半全纯Chern-Simons理论构造2维可积场论的同调方法开发了显式计算工具，通过循环L∞-代数的同伦转移实现谱曲线C的积分，并应用于主手征模型（含Wess-Zumino项）的Maurer-Cartan作用量与Lax联络的重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为基于同调方法从4维半全纯Chern-Simons理论构造2维可积场论提供显式的、可计算的操作工具，填补该框架在具体计算实现上的空白。

Method: 构造了C=ℂP¹上除子扭曲Dolbeault复形的强形变收缩，将谱曲线C的积分操作实现为循环L∞-代数的同伦转移，并以此使转移后的L∞-结构可计算。

Result: 成功计算了对应于含Wess-Zumino项的主手征模型的转移Maurer-Cartan作用量，该作用量重求和后还原为标准的主手征模型作用量；同时推导出的Lax联络与已知结果一致。

Conclusion: 证明了该同调框架可用于实际计算，并验证了其与已知可积场论（主手征模型）的一致性，为更广泛的2维可积场论构造提供了有效的代数工具。

Abstract: We develop explicit computational tools for the recent homological approach to the construction of $2$-dimensional integrable field theories on $Σ$ from $4$-dimensional semi-holomorphic Chern-Simons theory on $Σ\times C$. In this framework, the operation of integrating out the spectral curve $C$ is realized by homotopy transfer of a cyclic $L_\infty$-algebra associated with the $4$-dimensional theory with prescribed singularities and boundary conditions. We construct explicit strong deformation retracts for divisor-twisted Dolbeault complexes on $C=\mathbb{C}P^1$ and use them to make the transferred $L_\infty$-structure computationally accessible. As an application, we study the choice of meromorphic $1$-form corresponding to the principal chiral model with a Wess-Zumino term. We compute the transferred Maurer-Cartan action and the associated Lax connection, showing that the former resums to the standard principal chiral model action with a Wess-Zumino term and that the latter reproduces the usual Lax connection.

</details>


### [137] [Liouville strings in AdS$_3$: the worldsheet story](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12103)
*Gaston Giribet,Pedro Schmied*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS$_3$上带NS-NS通量的弦论世界面$σ$-模型，该模型可等效地描述为Liouville场论与一个带背景荷的类时场加上边缘形变的耦合。产生形变的算符是非归一化的，在弦论视角下与谱流扇区$ω=2$相关联，其作用是控制散射振幅中的卷绕数破坏。在半经典（大$k$）极限下，Liouville因子的中心荷趋于对偶CFT$_2$的中心荷，即$c\simeq 6k$。这与对偶变形orbifold CFT$_2$的Liouville型描述有相似之处（那里也出现了一个由非归一化场修饰的twist-2形变算符），但也存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$对偶中NS-NS通量背景下弦论世界面理论与对偶CFT之间更深层的联系，特别是通过Liouville场论这一共同语言来比较两者中形变算符的角色和性质。

Method: 将AdS$_3$上的弦世界面$σ$-模型等价映射为Liouville场论耦合带背景荷的类时场及边缘形变；分析该形变算符（非归一化，与谱流扇区$ω=2$关联）在散射振幅中的作用；在半经典大$k$极限下比较Liouville中心荷与对偶CFT的中心荷；并与对偶变形orbifold CFT$_2$的Liouville描述进行对比。

Result: 该形变算符是非归一化的，与谱流扇区$ω=2$相关，并负责控制散射振幅中的卷绕数破坏。在大$k$极限下，Liouville因子的中心荷$c\simeq 6k$与对偶CFT$_2$的中心荷一致。尽管在形式上与变形orbifold CFT$_2$中的twist-2形变算符有相似之处，但两者存在本质差异。

Conclusion: AdS$_3$弦论世界面的Liouville描述与对偶CFT的Liouville型描述之间有相似性，但在形变算符的具体性质上存在重要差异，这些差异对于深入理解AdS/CFT对偶中微观机制的细节具有关键意义。

Abstract: The worldsheet $σ$-model of strings on AdS$_3$ with NS-NS fluxes can be equivalently described in terms of a Liouville field theory coupled to a timelike field with background charge, plus a marginal deformation. The operator that produces the deformation is non-normalizable and, from the string theory perspective, is associated to the spectral flow sector $ω=2$. Its role is to control the winding number violation in scattering amplitudes. In the semiclassical (large $k$) limit, the central charge of the Liouville factor tends to that of the dual CFT$_2$, namely $c\simeq 6k$. This may appear very similar to what occurs in the Liouville-type description of the dual deformed orbifold CFT$_2$, where a twist-2 deformation operator dressed with a non-normalized field also appears. Indeed, there are some similarities to that; however, there are also significant differences, which we discuss.

</details>


### [138] [Non-Abelian $A_4$ vortices in $SO(3)$ gauge theory and non-invertible symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12366)
*Yoshihiko Abe,Tetsutaro Higaki,Kazuya Murakami,Muneto Nitta,Ryo Yokokura*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在可重整化的SO(3)规范理论中构造了有限张力的非阿贝尔涡旋解，该理论通过自旋3表示的希格斯场破缺到四面体群A₄。涡旋由二元四面体群\(\widetilde{A}_4\)分类，数值计算了A₄二阶和三阶共轭类的涡旋张力，展示了I型、II型和BPS-like行为，并证明这些光滑有限张力涡旋在红外退化为A₄离散规范理论中的Gukov-Witten表面算符，为不可逆缺陷提供了有限能量的紫外完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为离散规范理论中的非可逆缺陷（如Gukov-Witten表面算符）提供可重整化量子场论中的有限能量紫外完成，构造非阿贝尔涡旋并研究其分类和张力行为。

Method: 在SO(3)规范理论中引入自旋3表示的希格斯场，将规范群破缺到A₄；利用基本群\(\pi_1(SO(3)/A_4)\simeq \widetilde{A}_4\)对涡旋进行分类；通过数值方法求解轴对称涡旋解并计算张力，分析其随希格斯质量和规范玻色子质量比的变化行为。

Result: 获得了携带A₄二阶和三阶共轭类完整性的显式轴对称涡旋解；数值确定了张力；发现涡旋表现出I型、II型和BPS-like行为，取决于质量比；涡旋由\(\widetilde{A}_4\)共轭类分类，红外描述由A₄共轭类标记；光滑涡旋在红外极限下退化为A₄离散规范理论中的Gukov-Witten表面算符。

Conclusion: 成功在可重整化规范-希格斯理论中构建了非阿贝尔涡旋的有限能量紫外完成，建立了与离散规范理论中不可逆缺陷的联系，为理解非可逆对称性和表面算符提供了具体场论实现。

Abstract: We construct finite-tension non-Abelian vortex solutions in a renormalizable $(3+1)$-dimensional $SO(3)$ gauge theory Higgsed to the tetrahedral group $A_4$ by a Higgs field in the spin-3 representation. Since the vacuum manifold is $SO(3)/A_4$, the vortices are characterized by the non-Abelian fundamental group $π_1(SO(3)/A_4)\simeq \widetilde{A}_4$, the binary tetrahedral group. We obtain explicit axisymmetric vortex solutions carrying holonomies corresponding to the order-two and order-three conjugacy classes of $A_4$, determine their tensions numerically, and show that they exhibit type-I, type-II, and Bogomol'nyi--Prasad--Sommerfield-like behavior depending on the Higgs and gauge boson mass ratios. The vortices are classified by conjugacy classes of $\widetilde{A}_4$, while their infrared descriptions are labeled by conjugacy classes of $A_4$. We further demonstrate that the smooth finite-tension vortices reduce in the infrared to Gukov--Witten surface operators of the $A_4$ discrete gauge theory, thereby establishing a finite-energy ultraviolet completion of non-invertible defects in a renormalizable gauge-Higgs theory.

</details>


### [139] [Conformal line defects from matter-coupled 5D N=4 gauged supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12566)
*Parinya Karndumri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维N=4规范超引力中的全息共形线缺陷解，分析了不同规范群（SO(2)_D×SO(3)、SO(2)×ISO(3)、SO(2)×SO(3)×SO(3）下AdS_5真空之间的缺陷解，并讨论了其对应的边界SCFT及M5-brane的起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究全息框架下共形线缺陷的解析结构，探索不同规范群对应的超对称AdS_5真空之间的插值解，以及这些解在六维N=(0,2)场论和N=2 SCFT中的物理实现。

Method: 利用五维N=4规范超引力，在SO(2)_D×SO(3)、SO(2)×ISO(3)、SO(2)×SO(3)×SO(3)三种规范群下构造全息线缺陷解；通过一致截断将解提升至十一维超引力，并分析解的渐近行为与超对称性。

Result: 发现只有渐近于N=4真空的解才可能对应N=2 SCFT中的线缺陷；SO(2)×ISO(3)规范群下得到从六维N=(0,2)场论流向N=2 SCFT的解；SO(2)×SO(3)×SO(3)规范群下找到连接两个N=4真空的线缺陷解。

Conclusion: 全息线缺陷解提供了在AdS/CFT对偶中描述共形缺陷的新视角，不同规范群结构对应不同维度的边界场论和M5-brane编织几何。

Abstract: We study a number of holographic solutions describing conformal line defects within $N=2$ SCFTs from matter-coupled $N=4$ gauged supergravity in five dimensions. For $SO(2)_D\times SO(3)$ gauge group, the gauged supergravity admits two supersymmetric $AdS_5$ vacua with $N=4$ and $N=2$ unbroken supersymmetries. It turns out that only solutions that are asymptotic to the $N=4$ vacuum are possible leading to line defects within the dual $N=2$ SCFT. In gauged supergravity with $SO(2)\times ISO(3)$ gauge group, we find similar solutions with the $N=2$ SCFT appearing in the IR while the UV field theories correspond to six-dimensional $N=(0,2)$ field theories arising from M5-branes wrapped on a hyperbolic space $H^2$. The solution with $SO(2)\times SO(3)$ symmetry is also uplifted to eleven dimensions via a consistent truncation on $H^2\times S^4$. Finally, for gauged supergravity with $SO(2)\times SO(3)\times SO(3)$ gauge group that admits two $N=4$ supersymmetric $AdS_5$ vacua, we find a solution describing a line defect that interpolates between these two vacua as well as a solution with only one asymptotic $AdS_5$ geometry.

</details>


### [140] [Off-shell equivalence in quantum field theory and gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12644)
*Iberê Kuntz,Stefano Liberati*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于Vilkovisky–DeWitt有效作用量，提出了一个操作性的离壳等价性判据，用于判定不同场论表述是否在量子层面上等价。该判据通过配置空间张量与可容许探针的配对构造标量可观测量，区分了局部等价、分支等价和全局等价，并指出metric f(R)引力及其辅助场形式在路径积分中实施约束时是等价的，但与标量-张量形式不等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准等价定理是壳上结果，无法判定两个量子描述在离壳时是否等价。这一问题在引力、宇宙学和非平衡量子场论中尤为关键，需要发展一个更严格的离壳等价性判据。

Method: 以Vilkovisky–DeWitt有效作用量为工具，通过配置空间张量与可容许探针的配对构造标量可观测量，建立操作性判据；以metric f(R)引力和标量场理论为例进行检验。

Result: 该判据能区分局部、分支和全局等价三种层次；metric f(R)引力与其辅助场形式在路径积分中施加约束时量子等价，但与独立积分标量-张量形式不等价；表观的不等价可归因于比较了不同的量子对象。

Conclusion: 提出了场重定义和辅助变量扩展下局部与全局等价的精确概念，澄清了量子不等价表象的来源。

Abstract: Field redefinitions connect many formulations of the same physics, but the standard equivalence theorem is an on-shell result and cannot be used to decide when two quantum descriptions are equivalent off shell. This paper develops an operational criterion for that problem in terms of the Vilkovisky--DeWitt effective action. The central idea is that equivalence should be tested on scalar observables built by pairing configuration-space tensors with admissible probes. This makes the criterion sensitive to the observable class under consideration and separates the usual on-shell notion of equivalence from the stronger off-shell notions needed in gravity, cosmology and non-equilibrium quantum field theory. Metric $f(R)$ gravity and a scalar field theory example serve as prototypes, showing how the formal criterion distinguishes local, branchwise and genuinely global equivalence. In particular, we show that metric $f(R)$ gravity and its auxiliary-field reformulation yield the same quantum theory when the auxiliary constraint is enforced in the path integral. This is distinct from quantizing the corresponding scalar--tensor action with the metric and scalar treated as independent integration variables, which defines a different quantum theory. Apparent quantum inequivalences can then be traced to comparisons between different quantum objects, rather than to a failure of actual equivalences. This leads to general and precise notions of local and global equivalence under both field redefinitions and auxiliary-variable extensions.

</details>


### [141] [Quantum States Prepared by Wormholes: Long-Wavelength Deviations from Bunch-Davies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12772)
*George Lavrelashvili,Jean-Luc Lehners*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了酒虫洞（wineglass wormholes）在从渐近AdS或平直时空孕育出婴儿宇宙的过程中，所准备的张量和探测标量场的量子态。研究发现，短波长模式重现Bunch-Davies真空，但大尺度上存在偏离，这些偏离依赖于虫洞电荷，会导致暴胀阶段产生的角功率谱出现微小偏移和振荡，从而提供独特的宇宙学可观测信号。在电荷趋零时发生拓扑相变，小电荷极限下偏离被压制，表明相变是光滑的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究虫洞几何所准备的量子涨落态，特别是其与Bunch-Davies真空的偏差，以及这些偏差如何产生可观测的宇宙学信号。

Method: 分析酒虫洞（由轴子或磁荷支撑）中张量和探测标量场的量子态，对比短波长与大尺度模式，研究电荷参数对偏差的影响以及电荷趋零时的拓扑相变行为。

Result: 短波长模式重现Bunch-Davies真空；大尺度上存在与虫洞电荷相关的偏离（偏移和振荡）；小电荷极限下偏离被压制，拓扑相变光滑。

Conclusion: 酒虫洞为婴儿宇宙的孕育提供了可观测的宇宙学印记，其导致的角功率谱偏差可作为检验该机制的独特探针。

Abstract: Wineglass wormholes mediate the nucleation of baby universes out of an asymptotically Anti-de Sitter or flat spacetime. Upon materialization, the new universe naturally undergoes an inflationary phase. Here we study the quantum state of tensor and probe scalar field fluctuations that these wormhole geometries prepare, finding that they reproduce the Bunch-Davies vacuum for short-wavelength modes but lead to deviations from Bunch-Davies on large scales. These deviations, which depend on the charge of the wormhole, cause a small shift as well as oscillations in the angular power spectrum generated by an ensuing inflationary phase, and thus provide a distinctive cosmological observable.
  The wormholes are supported by either axionic or magnetic charges. In the limit of vanishing charge, they undergo a topological transition after which they contain no-boundary instantons. We find that the small-charge limit suppresses deviations from the Bunch-Davies state for both scalar and tensor fluctuations, indicating that the topological transition is smooth.

</details>


### [142] [On interacting non-Abelian antisymmetric tensor field models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12798)
*I. Buchbinder,N. Kozyrev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究通过群流形维数约简得到的相互作用反对称张量场模型的规范结构，发现相互作用模型中规范变换的可约化阶段与相应的自由模型相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决相互作用反对称张量场模型中规范变换可约性的开放问题，明确相互作用是否改变规范变换的可约化结构。

Method: 通过群流形上的维数约简构建相互作用模型，分析其规范结构，比较相互作用模型与自由模型在规范变换可约化阶段上的异同。

Result: 相互作用模型的规范变换可约化阶段与对应的自由模型完全一致。

Conclusion: 在通过群流形维数约简得到的反对称张量场模型中，相互作用（由紧致流形尺寸相关的维度参数作为耦合常数引入）不会改变规范变换的可约化结构，保留了自由理论的可约化特性。

Abstract: We study the gauge structure of interacting antisymmetric tensor field models obtained by the dimensional reduction on a group manifold. In such models a dimensionful parameter related to the size of the compact manifold plays the role of a coupling constant. We focus on open issues of reducibility of the gauge transformations in these theories and show that stages of reducibility of the interacting models coincide with ones of corresponding free counterparts.

</details>


### [143] [Timelike Entanglement First Law and Linearized Field Equations in Higher Curvature Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12834)
*Mei-Hui Xiao,Guo-Ying Li,Song He,Jia-Rui Sun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Lovelock引力对偶的全息共形场论中的类时间纠缠第一定律。通过双Wick转动公式和Jacobson-Myers熵泛函，计算了双曲子区域的全息类时间纠缠熵的线性变分。对于三次Lovelock引力，发现单一普适的重整化因子同时控制熵和模哈密顿量的高阶曲率修正，从而在低能热激发下保持ΔS=Δ⟨H⟩。进一步推广到任意阶Lovelock引力，发现对于可归一化扰动，Jacobson-Myers泛函的变分等价于爱因斯坦引力结果乘以一个耦合依赖因子，该因子也重整化了线性化场方程中的有效牛顿常数。边界项在共形极限下消失，从而类时间纠缠第一定律等价于Lovelock引力在最大对称背景下的线性化场方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高阶曲率引力（Lovelock引力）中全息类时间纠缠熵是否满足纠缠第一定律，以及高阶曲率修正如何影响熵与模哈密顿量之间的变分关系。

Method: 采用双Wick转动方法计算类时间纠缠熵，结合Jacobson-Myers熵泛函，在Fefferman-Graham规范下对Lovelock引力进行线性变分分析，并考察边界项的贡献。

Result: 证明了在Lovelock引力中，类时间纠缠熵的变分与模哈密顿量的变分之间仍满足ΔS=Δ⟨H⟩关系，且高阶曲率修正通过一个普适重整化因子体现；该因子也出现在线性化场方程的有效牛顿常数中；边界项在共形极限下为零。

Conclusion: 类时间纠缠第一定律在Lovelock引力中成立，且等价于最大对称背景上的线性化场方程，表明纠缠第一定律与引力动力学之间存在深刻的对应关系，这一关系在更一般的引力理论中依然成立。

Abstract: We investigate the timelike entanglement first law in holographic conformal field theories whose bulk dual is Lovelock gravity. Using the double Wick rotation formulation of timelike entanglement entropy together with the Jacobson-Myers entropy functional, we compute the linear variation of holographic timelike entanglement entropy for hyperbolic subregions. For cubic Lovelock gravity, we explicitly show that a single, universal multiplicative renormalization factor governs how higher curvature interactions enter the variations of both the entropy and the modular Hamiltonian, leading to $ΔS=Δ\langle H\rangle$ for low-energy thermal excitations. We then extend the analysis to Lovelock gravity of arbitrary order around the anti-de Sitter spacetime in the Fefferman-Graham gauge. For normalizable perturbations, the variation of the Jacobson-Myers functional reduces to the Einstein gravity's result multiplied by the same coupling-dependent factor that renormalizes the effective Newtonian constant in the linearized field equations of Lovelock gravity. We further show that the boundary contribution vanishes in the conformal limit for the class of perturbations considered. Consequently, the timelike entanglement first law is equivalent to the linearized field equations of Lovelock gravity about the maximally symmetric background, within the hyperbolic and perturbative regime considered in the present paper.

</details>


### [144] [Thermodynamic scaling and string dynamics in rotating holographic QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12906)
*Leila Shahkarami,Farid Charmchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton全息QCD框架下研究有限角速度对相结构和Schwinger对产生的影响。旋转通过boost构造实现，发现热力学相边界满足精确标度关系，但禁闭-退禁闭弦相边界不满足该关系；旋转降低了有效弦张力、临界电场和势垒，增强了Schwinger对产生，并在退禁闭相中产生世界sheet视界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有限角速度在全息QCD模型中对相变、禁闭和Schwinger对产生等非微扰现象的物理效应，特别是旋转在热力学和弦动力学两个层面上的不同作用机制。

Method: 基于Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton框架构建全息QCD模型，通过boost构造引入旋转生成平稳旋转几何；用巨势和响应函数研究热力学相图；用有效弦张力研究禁闭；用势分析方法研究Schwinger效应。

Result: 热力学相边界满足精确标度关系，旋转完全由静态解的boost变换决定；弦禁闭-退禁闭相边界不满足标度关系，热力学禁闭但弦退禁闭的区域随角速度增大；旋转降低临界电场和势垒，增强对产生；退禁闭相中旋转产生世界sheet视界，减少最大夸克-反夸克分离和最深深点。

Conclusion: 旋转在热力学扇区通过精确标度律表现为平凡变换，但在弦扇区产生真正的新物理效应，两者行为不对称。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of finite angular velocity on the phase structure and Schwinger pair production in a holographic QCD model based on the Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton framework. Rotation is introduced through a boost construction that generates a stationary rotating geometry from the corresponding static background. We first study the thermodynamic phase diagram using the grand potential together with several response functions and show that all thermodynamic phase boundaries satisfy an exact scaling relation under rotation, implying that the rotating thermodynamic sector is completely determined by the corresponding static solution through a simple boost transformation. We then investigate confinement through the effective string tension and find that, consistent with the thermodynamic observables, rotation decreases the string confinement--deconfinement transition temperature and chemical potential. The corresponding phase boundary, however, no longer obeys the thermodynamic scaling relation. Consequently, the region in the phase diagram that is thermodynamically confined but string deconfined grows with angular velocity. We further study the Schwinger effect using the potential analysis approach. Rotation lowers both the confining and catastrophic critical electric fields and suppresses the height and width of the potential barrier, thereby enhancing pair production in both confined and deconfined phases. In the deconfined phase, rotation also generates a worldsheet horizon that limits the radial extent of connected string solutions and reduces both the maximum quark--antiquark separation and the deepest turning point of the energetically favored string. These results demonstrate that, while rotation acts trivially in the thermodynamic sector through an exact scaling law, it produces genuinely new effects in the string sector.

</details>


### [145] [Holography from number theory: Emergent holographic AdS$_2$ space-time from exact BPS black hole microstate counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12923)
*Gabriel Lopes Cardoso,Suresh Nampuri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从4D BPS黑洞简并度的精确计数公式出发，通过Rademacher展开与DFF共形量子力学模型中Segal-Bargmann热核计算的对应，证明了BPS贝肯斯坦-霍金熵及其对数幂律修正的全息起源，并推导出二维全息对偶的AdS$_2$时空，展示了熵从纠缠熵计算中涌现的过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从精确数论简并度公式出发，揭示4D BPS黑洞微观状态计数的全息起源，建立黑洞熵与共形量子力学之间的精确对应关系。

Method: 利用1/2 BPS $\mathcal{N}=4$和1/8 BPS $\mathcal{N}=8$解精确计数公式的Rademacher展开，将其与DFF共形量子力学模型中的Segal-Bargmann热核计算建立数论数据层面的对应，进而推导全息对偶的AdS$_2$时空并计算纠缠熵。

Result: 证明了BPS贝肯斯坦-霍金熵及其所有对数幂律修正项的全息起源；导出了与DFF模型能量尺度相关的二维全息对偶AdS$_2$时空；展示了贝肯斯坦-霍金熵从该时空纠缠熵计算中涌现的过程。

Conclusion: 对于两类4D BPS黑洞，从精确数论简并度公式推断出与微观状态计数相关的全息CQM，并证明了近 horizon AdS$_2$吸引子几何的全息对偶性。

Abstract: In this note, starting from the exact counting formulae for 4D BPS black hole degeneracies in the cases of 1/2 BPS $\mathcal{N}=4$ and 1/8 BPS $\mathcal{N}=8$ solutions, expressed as Rademacher expansions for coefficients of modular and Jacobi forms respectively, we explicitly show how the number theoretic data in each Rademacher summand is encoded by a Segal-Bargmann heat kernel calculation in the de Alfaro-Fubini-Furlan (DFF) model of conformal quantum mechanics, thus demonstrating the holographic origin of the BPS Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, and all logarithmic and power law suppressed corrections to it. We then derive the 2D holographically dual bulk space-time for this CQM and show that it is an AdS$_2$ space-time with radial length scale correlated to the energy scale of the DFF model. We further show how the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy emerges from an entanglement entropy computation in this bulk space-time. Hence, for the two classes of 4D BPS black holes under consideration, we have inferred the holographic CQM relevant for microscopic state counting from the exact number-theoretic degeneracy formulae and demonstrated the emergence of the near-horizon AdS$_2$ attractor geometry that is holographically dual to it.

</details>


### [146] [Recursion Relations for Classical Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12973)
*Poul H. Damgaard,Kwangeon Kim,Kanghoon Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了一套递归关系，用于在爱因斯坦引力中微扰评估两个黑洞的散射，并在第三后闵可夫斯基阶恢复了包括引力辐射反作用在内的正确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在爱因斯坦引力框架下精确计算两个黑洞散射的微扰展开，需要发展一套系统的递归方法，以处理高阶后闵可夫斯基（post-Minkowskian）修正。

Method: 推导了一组递归关系，用于微扰评估两个黑洞在爱因斯坦引力中的散射，并在第三后闵可夫斯基阶求解了这些方程。

Result: 在第三后闵可夫斯基阶恢复了正确结果，包括引力辐射的反作用效应。

Conclusion: 该递归关系为高阶后闵可夫斯基计算提供了系统框架，并且成功验证了其正确性。

Abstract: We derive a set of recursion relations for the perturbative evaluation of the scattering of two black holes in Einstein gravity. To illustrate, we solve the equations up to third post-Minkowskian order in the gravitational coupling constant G, recovering the correct result, including the back-reaction from gravitational radiation.

</details>


### [147] [Causality and realizability of local operations in quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12976)
*Jan Mandrysch,Robin Simmons,Miguel Navascues*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子场论中Sorkin悖论及其解决方案，证明了存在因果通道无法通过FV方案任意逼近，且判定仪器是否可FV实现是不可计算问题。同时指出一类广泛的因果仪器（随机位移测量通道）是FV可实现的，可用于实现非破坏性量子测量和概率性近似任意QFT操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Sorkin悖论对量子场论中局域操作的限制问题，比较两种因果性方案（Jubb-Oeckl与Fewster-Verch）的兼容性，探究FV可实现仪器的适用范围与局限性。

Method: 在自由标量场量子场论框架下，比较Jubb-Oeckl因果条件与Fewster-Verch的FV可实现方案；通过构造反例证明存在因果通道无法被FV方案逼近；利用不可计算性论证判定问题；证明随机位移通道的FV可实现性及其应用。

Result: (1) 存在因果通道无法被FV方案任意逼近；(2) 判定给定仪器是否FV可实现是不可计算问题；(3) 随机位移通道类可实现任意场正交算子的非破坏性POVM；(4) 可概率性（ heralded ）近似任意QFT操作（无论因果与否）。

Conclusion: FV方案虽能确保因果性但存在根本局限性（不可逼近性与不可判定性），而随机位移通道构成一类足够丰富且FV可实现的因果仪器，为局域QFT操作提供了实用框架。

Abstract: In 1993, Sorkin noted, in the context of quantum field theory (QFT), that identifying the operations accessible to an observer acting in some spacetime region with quantum instruments whose Kraus operators are localizable therein leads to superluminal communication. This so-called Sorkin paradox can be resolved by further constraining the set of allowed local operations in QFT. In this spirit, Jubb and Oeckl identified the minimal conditions that QFT instruments must satisfy to be compatible with Einstein's causality. Independently, Fewster and Verch proposed a framework for local QFT operations that generalizes non-relativistic quantum measurement theory; remarkably, such FV-realizable instruments were shown to be causal. In this work, we study both approaches in the quantum field theory of the free scalar field. In this regard, we prove that there exist causal channels that cannot be arbitrarily well approximated through FV schemes. Moreover, deciding whether a given instrument is implementable or far away from being implementable by composing several FV operations is an uncomputable problem. On a more optimistic note, we show that a very wide class of causal instruments is FV-realizable: namely, those whose measurement channels are random displacements of the field operators. As we show, those suffice to implement any Positive Operator Valued Measure over a set of field quadratures in a non-demolition way (i.e., without perturbing any other commuting quadratures). Similarly, they allow approximating arbitrary QFT operations, causal or not, in a heralded, probabilistic way.

</details>


### [148] [Enhanced Correlations in Hawking Radiation from Near-Extremal Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12989)
*Suzanne Bintanja,Seolhwa Kim,Per Kraus*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过约化到s波并采用爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论与标量场的哈密顿形式，研究了近极端Reissner-Nordstrom黑洞在坍缩过程中的形成，展示了如何计算由于近视界区域增强的引力反作用效应导致的出射Hawking辐射中的关联。通过求解约束，得到了在量子层面包含引力反作用效应的标量场作用量，其有效耦合g=G/(πr_0^3 T_H)在低温下增长。该作用量对自由场Hawking态产生修正，并烙印在无穷远处Hawking辐射的关联函数上。研究表明，该作用量在AdS_2区域评估时，在树图边界关联函数层面等价于标准JT/Schwarzian描述耦合 dressed双局域算符，并重现了单圈结果。该方法将度规涨落通过约化标量作用量以量子力学方式包含，而非半经典期望值，当忽略度规涨落时，辐射计算明显约化为Hawking的原始处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近极端Reissner-Nordstrom黑洞的Hawking辐射中，由于近视界区域增强的引力反作用效应，自由场近似不足以描述量子关联。本文旨在发展一种量子力学框架，能够自洽地纳入引力反作用，从而计算由此产生的出射辐射关联修正。

Method: 采用s波约化和爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量场系统的哈密顿形式；通过求解约束获得包含量子引力反作用效应的约化标量场作用量；该作用量与AdS_2区域的JT/Schwarzian理论及dressed双局域算符进行等效性分析；通过关联函数计算验证修正效应。

Result: 得到了在量子层面包含引力反作用效应的标量场有效作用量，其耦合常数在低温下发散；该作用量在树图级别等价于标准JT/Schwarzian描述；成功重现部分单圈结果；当忽略度规涨落时，结果约化为标准Hawking辐射。

Conclusion: 本文发展的哈密顿约化方法能够系统处理近极端黑洞Hawking辐射中的引力反作用效应，为超越半经典近似的量子黑洞辐射计算提供了新框架，且与AdS_2/CFT_1对偶框架下的已知结果一致。

Abstract: We consider the formation of a near-extremal Reissner-Nordstrom black hole by collapse, and show how to compute correlations in the outgoing Hawking radiation due to enhanced gravitational backreaction effects in the near-horizon region. This is done by reducing to the s-wave and employing the Hamiltonian formulation of Einstein-Maxwell theory coupled to a scalar field. Solving the constraints yields an action for the scalar field that incorporates gravitational backreaction effects at the quantum level, governed by an effective coupling g = G/(πr_0^3 T_H) that grows at low temperature, as in recent Schwarzian-based analyses. This action produces corrections to the free field Hawking state which are imprinted on correlation functions of the Hawking radiation measured at null infinity. As part of our analysis, we show that this action evaluated in the AdS_2 region is equivalent, at the level of all tree-level boundary correlators, to the standard JT/Schwarzian description coupled to dressed bilocal operators. We also reproduce some one-loop results. In our approach, metric fluctuations are included quantum mechanically through the reduced scalar action, rather than through a semiclassical expectation value, and our computation of the radiation manifestly reduces to Hawking's original treatment when metric fluctuations are neglected.

</details>


### [149] [Compact, long-range and vacuumless regimes controlled by scalar fields in two-dimensional flat and five-dimensional warped geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12119)
*Elisama E. M. Lima,Dionisio Bazeia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了实标量场在2D平坦和5D翘曲几何中的三种存在区域（紧凑、长程和无真空），发现2D模型中的局域解嵌入到翘曲AdS₅背景时会生成不同种类的膜轮廓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索实标量场在不同维度几何（2D平坦和5D翘曲AdS₅）中的紧凑、长程和无真空区域的存在性，以及这些区域如何影响膜的轮廓。

Method: 在2D情况下详细研究多个支持局域解的模型，并将其嵌入到翘曲的AdS₅背景中，分析由此产生的不同膜轮廓。

Result: 2D平坦几何中的局域解嵌入到5D翘曲AdS₅背景时，会产生不同类型（紧凑、长程、无真空）的膜轮廓。

Conclusion: 实标量场在不同维度几何中可呈现紧凑、长程和无真空三种区域，且2D模型的局域解在嵌入5D翘曲空间后会影响膜结构形态。

Abstract: We investigate the presence of compact, long-range, and vacuumless regimes in models described by real scalar fields in 2D flat and 5D warped geometries. In the two-dimensional case, we study in detail several models that support localized solutions. When embedded into a warped $\text{AdS}_5$ background, these fields generate distinct kinds of brane profiles.

</details>

### [150] [Beyond ΛCDM with the SKA Observatory -- I: Probing Gravity on Cosmological Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11917)
*S. Camera,C. Addis,S. L. Guedezounme,F. Montano,T. Montandon,M. Novara,S. J. Rossiter,J. Asorey,S. Castello,C. Clarkson,J. Fonseca,N. Frusciante,C. Gomes,N. Grimm,A. Nasirudin,L. G. T. Oliveira,F. Pace,Z. Sakr,M. Berti,C. Bonvin,P. Bull,E. Di Dio,C. Cress,C. Heneka,H. -R. Kloeckner,R. Maartens,C. J. A. P. Martins,D. Parkinson,C. S. Saraf*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文讨论了广义相对论在宇宙学尺度上测试的必要性，并指出SKA观测站因其探测体积和多种宇宙学观测手段，非常适合进行此类测试，以区分暗物质/暗能量是真实存在还是引力理论不完备的产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然广义相对论在强场区域已得到精确验证，但在宇宙学尺度上（弱引力场）我们看到了暗物质和暗能量的需求，这引发了这些" exotic components "是真实存在还是GR在宇宙学尺度上不完备的表现的问题，因此需要测试GR在这些尺度上的有效性。

Method: 利用SKA（平方千米阵列）天文台的大体积探测能力和多种互补的宇宙学观测手段，在宇宙学尺度上检验广义相对论的有效性。

Result: SKA天文台因其巨大的探测体积和多样化的宇宙学可观测量的互补性，特别适合用于测试GR在宇宙学尺度上的有效性。

Conclusion: 为检验GR在宇宙学尺度上的有效性，SKA天文台是最理想的观测工具，其观测结果有助于确认GR的稳健性或发现偏差，从而推动更普适的引力理论的建立。

Abstract: General relativity (GR) is currently the best description of the gravitational interaction at our disposal and is one of the foundations of the concordance cosmological model. For as much as we know that GR is not the final theory of gravitation - we still lack an understanding of its fundamental, quantum nature - it has demonstrated a remarkable success in describing observed phenomena and predicting effects that have later been confirmed by laboratory experiments or astronomical observations. Since gravity is extremely weak compared to the other three fundamental interactions, it has so far been tested with exquisite precision only in the strong-field regime. On the immense scales of the cosmos, on the other hand, the gravitational field is extremely weak and spacetime curvature is almost negligible. But crucially, it is on these scales that we see hints at the need for exotic components, such as dark matter and dark energy. The question of whether they really exist or their presence is but an artefact of the incompleteness of our understanding of gravity on cosmological scales then naturally arises. It is therefore paramount to test the validity of GR on these scales, either to further confirm its robustness or to detect deviations that could lead us to the formulation of a more general and conclusive theory of gravitation. To this purpose, the SKA Observatory is especially suited, thanks both to the enormous volumes it will probe, and to the variety and complementarity of cosmological observables that its surveys will make available to us.

</details>


### [151] [Optical and thermodynamic properties of Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11979)
*Hassan Hassanabadi,Michael R. R. Good,Soroush Zare,Orlando Luongo,Fariba Kafikang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞在固定角动量系综下的热力学和光学性质，包括热力学量、弱场修正、有效AdS类热力学解释、Komar质量和电荷，以及光子轨道、阴影边界等光学性质，发现Bertotti-Robinson背景会减小平均遍历层厚度和阴影面积，增大光子-遍历层间隙，并产生负的阴影磁化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞（即浸没在外部Bertotti-Robinson电磁背景中的旋转黑洞）的热力学与光学性质，理解外部电磁背景对黑洞热力学和光学观测量的影响。

Method: 在固定角动量系综下导出热力学量；采用有效AdS类热力学解释处理Bertotti-Robinson尺度；利用Hamilton-Jacobi formalism推导分离的零测地线势、光子轨道影响参数和有限距离观测者的阴影边界坐标。

Result: 热力学量在B→0时平滑过渡到Kerr情况；弱场下热力学修正以B²阶主导；极值半径在B²阶移动，而残余质量在B⁴阶才有首阶修正；Bertotti-Robinson背景减小平均遍历层厚度和阴影面积，增大光子-遍历层间隙，产生负的阴影磁化率且其大小随旋转增强。

Conclusion: 建立了Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞系统的热力学和光学性质，揭示了外部电磁背景对黑洞可观测量的影响规律，为理解此类黑洞的物理特征提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic and optical properties of Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson black holes, namely rotating black holes immersed in an external Bertotti--Robinson electromagnetic background. In the fixed-$a$ ensemble, we derive the horizon mass relation, the Hawking temperature, the entropy, the Helmholtz-type free energy, the heat capacity, and the extremal remnant configuration. These quantities reduce smoothly to their Kerr counterparts as $B\to0$. In the weak-field regime, the leading thermodynamic corrections arise at order $B^2$; the extremal radius is shifted at this order, whereas the remnant mass receives its first correction only at order $B^4$. We also introduce a formal AdS-like thermodynamic interpretation of the Bertotti--Robinson scale, treating the associated pressure as an effective response variable rather than a genuine cosmological pressure. Because the spacetime is not asymptotically flat, we further compute the finite-radius Komar mass and the Komar charge associated with the horizon generator. Using the Hamilton--Jacobi formalism, we derive the separated null-geodesic potentials, the impact parameters of spherical photon orbits, and the celestial coordinates of the shadow boundary for a finite-distance observer. We then characterize the photon-region boundaries, ergosphere thickness, photon--ergosphere gap, shadow area, and magnetic shadow susceptibility. Within the perturbative regime considered, the Bertotti--Robinson background decreases the averaged ergosphere thickness and shadow area, increases the photon--ergosphere gap, and produces a negative shadow susceptibility whose magnitude is enhanced by rotation.

</details>


### [152] [Geometric effects of torsion on black hole ringdown and shadows in Poincaré gauge gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11989)
*Terkaa Victor Targema,Kazuharu Bamba,Riasat Ali,Usman Zafar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在庞加莱规范引力框架下研究了一类由时空挠率产生有效电荷的Reissner-Nordström-like黑洞，发现该黑洞仅具有单视界结构，且挠率增加会降低准正规模频率和衰减率，产生更长寿的环信号；通过EHT和LVK观测数据约束了挠率参数，结果与Schwarzschild极限一致，但挠率会在阴影和环信号上留下相关印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空挠率提供了广义相对论的自然扩展，可能导致与爱因斯坦引力显著不同的黑洞解，因此有必要研究挠率对黑洞物理和可观测量的影响。

Method: 采用六阶WKB近似、Leaver连分数法和eikonal对应关系研究标量扰动和自旋-2测试场；结合EHT对Sgr A*和M87*的观测以及LVK环信号数据，通过profile-χ²分析和蒙特卡洛采样约束挠率参数。

Result: 挠率增加会降低振荡频率和衰减率，产生更长寿命的环信号；观测约束与Schwarzschild极限在现有不确定度内一致。

Conclusion: 时空挠率在黑洞阴影和环信号上留下相关印记，但当前观测精度尚无法区分挠率修正与Schwarzschild黑洞。

Abstract: Spacetime torsion provides a natural extension of general relativity and may lead to black hole solutions that differ significantly from their Einsteinian counterparts. We investigate a class of Reissner-Nordström-like black holes in Poincaré gauge gravity, where the effective charge is generated entirely by spacetime torsion instead of an electromagnetic field. Within the physically relevant torsion sector, the spacetime exhibits a single-horizon structure, free from the inner horizons and extremal states characteristic of charged black holes. Using the sixth-order Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB) approximation, Leaver's continued-fraction method, and the eikonal correspondence between quasinormal modes and unstable null geodesics, we study scalar perturbations and spin-2 test fields on the torsion-modified background. We find that increasing torsion decreases both the oscillation frequencies and damping rates, leading to longer-lived ringdown signals. We further compare the model predictions with Event Horizon Telescope observations of Sgr A* and M87*, along with representative LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) ringdown scales, to derive constraints on the torsion parameter via a profile-$χ^{2}$ analysis supplemented by Monte Carlo sampling. Although the resulting bounds remain consistent with the Schwarzschild limit within current observational uncertainties, our results show that spacetime torsion leaves correlated imprints on both black hole shadows and ringdown observables.

</details>


### [153] [A new non-commutative correction to the thermodynamics and evaporation of the Schwarzschild black hole in non-commutative gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11991)
*Slimane Zaim,Fatma Zohra Bara*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过Seiberg-Witten映射得到的非对易修正对变形Schwarzschild黑洞热力学性质的影响，发现黑洞存在三个热力学相（两个不稳定相和一个稳定相），且非对易几何中的Schwarzschild黑洞寿命比经典情形更长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非对易几何修正对黑洞热力学性质的影响，特别是通过Seiberg-Witten映射得到的tetrad场非对易修正如何改变Schwarzschild黑洞的热力学行为。

Method: 通过Seiberg-Witten映射获得tetrad场的非对易修正，推导修正后的事件视界半径，进而计算霍金温度、熵、热容、亥姆霍兹自由能和压力等热力学量，并分析黑洞蒸发过程。

Result: 发现热容呈现丰富的热力学结构：两个具有负热容的不稳定相（大黑洞和小黑洞）之间夹着一个具有正热容的稳定中间相；非对易修正使黑洞寿命超过经典情形；引入了约10^-35米量级（普朗克长度量级）的特征长度尺度。

Conclusion: 非对易修正自然地引入了普朗克长度量级的基本长度尺度Θ=1.6×10^-35 m，改变了黑洞的热力学相结构和蒸发过程，延长了黑洞寿命。

Abstract: We investigate the modified thermodynamic properties of a deformed Schwarzschild black hole arising from new noncommutative corrections to the tetrad fields obtained through the Seiberg-Witten map. By deriving the corrected event horizon radius, we evaluate the corresponding thermodynamic quantities, including the Hawking temperature, entropy, heat capacity, Helmholtz free energy, and pressure. We also examine the evaporation process of the black hole within this noncommutative framework. The behavior of the heat capacity reveals a rich thermodynamic structure consisting of three distinct phases: two unstable phases with negative heat capacity, corresponding to a large black hole and a small black hole, separated by an intermediate stable phase with positive heat capacity. Our analysis also shows that the Schwarzschild black hole in noncommutative geometry possesses a finite lifetime that exceeds that of its classical counterpart, owing to the effective contribution of noncommutativity to the black hole mass. The noncommutative correction naturally introduces a fundamental length scale, Θ=1,6.10^-35 m, which is of the order of the Planck length.

</details>


### [154] [Spinning particle dynamics, epicyclic frequencies, and transient QPO signatures in Schwarzschild spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11993)
*Uktamjon Uktamov,Ali Övgün,Reggie C. Pantig,Bobomurat Ahmedov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Schwarzschild时空中，采用Mathisson–Papapetrou–Dixon pole–dipole近似和Tulczyjew–Dixon自旋补充条件，线性化研究了自旋试验粒子的运动，包括自旋修正的径向势、圆轨道条件、束缚周期轨道、周进频率、Lyapunov指数，并将结果应用于极端质量比旋进（EMRI）的引力波波形分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自旋–曲率耦合对强引力场中粒子运动的影响，为极端质量比旋进系统中自旋致密天体的引力波建模提供解析基础。

Method: 基于MPD方程，线性化自旋$s$，采用Levin–Perez-Giz zoom–whirl分类法构建周期轨道，利用相对论进动和共振方法分析准周期振荡，并通过Lyapunov指数与近同宿轨道分界线结构关联。

Result: 推导了ISCO位置$r_{\rm ISCO}=6M-2\sqrt{2/3}\,s+\mathcal{O}(s^2)$；获得了自旋对圆轨道能量、角动量、周进频率的修正；建立了Lyapunov指数与局部相空间分界线结构的解析联系；展示了自旋对引力波波形的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作为含线性自旋修正的MPD动力学、周期轨道分类、周进频率偏移以及强场瞬态现象提供了一个紧凑的解析框架，可直接应用于EMRI引力波建模。

Abstract: We study the motion of spinning test particles in Schwarzschild spacetime within the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon pole--dipole approximation, imposing the Tulczyjew--Dixon spin supplementary condition. Restricting to equatorial orbits with the particle spin aligned with the orbital angular momentum, and retaining terms through linear order in the specific spin $s$, we derive the spin-corrected radial potential, circular-orbit conditions, bound periodic trajectories, epicyclic frequencies, and Lyapunov exponents of unstable circular orbits. The spin--curvature coupling shifts the circular-orbit energy and angular momentum and moves the innermost stable circular orbit to $r_{\rm ISCO}=6M-2\sqrt{2/3}\,s+\mathcal{O}(s^2)$ in the sign convention adopted here. We construct bound periodic orbits using the Levin--Perez-Giz zoom--whirl taxonomy and show how the particle spin deforms the corresponding energy--angular-momentum map. We then obtain the coordinate-time azimuthal and radial epicyclic frequencies and use them as kinematical inputs for relativistic-precession and resonance prescriptions for quasi-periodic oscillations. Finally, we relate the Lyapunov exponent of unstable circular orbits to the local separatrix structure governing near-homoclinic zoom--whirl motion. The resulting formulation provides a compact analytic connection between linear-in-spin MPD dynamics, periodic-orbit taxonomy, epicyclic-frequency shifts, and transient strong-field phenomenology in a nonrotating black-hole background. Also, we study the gravitational waveforms from the periodic orbits of a massive spinning particle around a black hole, presenting those associated with extreme mass-ratio inspirals involving a stellar-mass compact spinning object orbiting a supermassive black hole.

</details>


### [155] [Thin-shell wormholes in cosmic voids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11995)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,Edson Otoniel,Francisco S. N. Lobo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在宇宙空洞背景下构建对称薄壳虫洞，通过Darmois-Israel条件推导表面应力，建立热力学第一定律，并分析两种Chaplygin气体状态方程下的稳定性，发现修正的宇宙Chaplygin气体支持的虫洞在某些参数下可以稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙空洞作为欠密区域具有德西特-like引力行为，且已有黑洞嵌入空洞密度分布的解，因此自然延伸探索在这种背景下构建薄壳虫洞的可能性及其稳定性。

Method: 采用Darmois-Israel连接条件计算表面应力，将能量条件用空洞质量函数和密度分布表示；推导静态壳的热力学第一定律；通过有效势分析径向动力学，并分别采用广义宇宙Chaplygin气体(GCCG)和修正宇宙Chaplygin气体(MCCG)状态方程进行稳定性测试。

Result: Chaplygin参数B由静态连接条件固定；GCCG支持的构型普遍不稳定；MCCG支持的虫洞在状态方程线性项足够大时可实现稳定。

Conclusion: 该框架连接了宇宙空洞的德西特-like结构与标准薄壳虫洞形式论，为识别位于空洞时空中黑洞视界与宇宙学视界之间稳定或非稳定的虫洞构型提供了起点。

Abstract: Cosmic voids are underdense regions that can provide an effective large-scale environment with a de Sitter-like gravitational behavior. Motivated by recent black-hole solutions embedded in void density profiles, we construct a symmetric thin-shell wormhole by gluing two copies of the positive-lapse region of a black hole inside a cosmic void. The surface stresses are obtained from the Darmois--Israel junction conditions, and the corresponding null, weak, dominant, and strong energy-condition combinations are written directly in terms of the void mass function and density profile.We further develop the thermodynamics of the static shell, deriving a first law that relates the shell entropy to the black-hole and cosmological-like horizon entropies.We then formulate the radial dynamics of the throat through an effective potential and study the local stability of static configurations when the exotic matter on the shell obeys either a generalized cosmic Chaplygin gas or a modified cosmic Chaplygin gas equation of state. In both models the Chaplygin parameter $B$ is fixed by the static junction condition, so that the remaining stability test is governed by the void geometry and by the equation-of-state parameters. Numerical results reveal that GCCG-supported configurations are generically unstable, whereas MCCG-supported wormholes can be stable for a sufficiently large linear term in the equation of state. The resulting framework connects the de Sitter-like structure of cosmic voids with the standard thin-shell wormhole formalism and provides a starting point for identifying stable or unstable wormhole configurations located between the black-hole and cosmological-like horizons of the void spacetime.

</details>


### [156] [No persuasive evidence yet of gravitational-wave tails from perturbers along the line of sight in LVK observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12043)
*Kuba Kopczuk,Ethan Blake,Matthew F. Carney,Jeremy G. Baier,Michael Van Keuren,Adam Khan,Leslie Wade,Madeline Wade,Glenn Starkman,Craig Copi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文搜索LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA前三次观测运行中90个致密双星并合事件附近的数据，寻找所谓的"引力闪烁"（引力波信号与时空扰动体相互作用产生的回波状引力波尾迹）。通过贝叶斯模型比较，未在高信噪比（≥12）的BBH或IMBH事件中发现令人信服的证据，并给出了闪烁相对振幅的上限以约束宇宙中扰动体的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证引力闪烁（gravitational glints）这一理论预测现象的存在性，利用现有引力波观测数据检验广义相对论在强场区域的预言，并探索时空扰动体的存在及其性质。

Method: 对LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA前三次观测运行中SNR≥12的BBH和IMBH事件附近数据，采用贝叶斯模型比较方法搜索引力闪烁信号；同时评估标准匹配滤波CBC搜索是否会遗漏含闪烁的信号。

Result: 未在90个事件中发现令人信服的引力闪烁证据；标准匹配滤波搜索不太可能遗漏含闪烁的信号；给出了闪烁相对振幅的概率上限，并用于约束宇宙中扰动体的性质。

Conclusion: 当前数据不支持引力闪烁的存在，但第四观测运行（O4）灵敏度提升后，有望探测到此类信号。

Abstract: Observations of compact binary coalescences (CBCs) by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA collaboration resulted in 90 events in the first three observing runs. We search the data near the loudest of these detections for so-called "gravitational glints," by performing Bayesian model comparison. Gravitational glints are gravitational-wave tails caused by interactions between a signal and a spacetime perturber and are theorized to present as echoes of the primary signal. We do not find convincing evidence of gravitational glints in binary black hole or intermediate-mass black hole binary events with a signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of at least 12. We also found that standard matched-filter CBC searches with template banks that do not contain gravitational glints would likely not have missed signals containing a gravitational glint. We therefore estimate upper limits on the probability of a glint as a function of its relative amplitude and use these estimates to constrain the properties of perturbers in the Universe. With increased sensitivity in the fourth observing run, which will increase the number and SNR of detectable events, we remain optimistic that glints will soon be detectable.

</details>


### [157] [High-order gravitational late-time tails in Kerr spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12151)
*Marc Casals,Chris Kavanagh,Jakob Neef,Adrian Ottewill*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了Kerr时空中Teukolsky方程推迟格林函数的高阶晚期尾项，包括有限半径、未来事件视界和未来零无穷远处的渐近行为，给出了自旋s的尾幂律和引力扰动(s=-2)的尾系数，并发现了高阶对数修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kerr黑洞扰动理论中，推迟格林函数的晚期尾行为对于理解黑洞响应、拟正则模衰减后的晚期信号至关重要。此前已知主导阶的幂律尾，但更高阶修正（特别是对数修正）尚未系统计算。

Method: 从频域推迟格林函数的傅里叶模出发，利用小频率展开技术，在三个区域（有限半径、未来事件视界、未来零无穷）分别导出大时间坐标下的渐近展开，得到前三个主导阶的尾项。

Result: 获得了有限半径处t^{-2ℓ-3}（主导）及t^{-2ℓ-5}ln t（次主导）；未来事件视界处e^{imΩ_H v}v^{-2ℓ-3-b}（主导）及e^{imΩ_H v}v^{-2ℓ-5-b}ln v（次主导）；未来零无穷处u^{-ℓ+s-2}（主导）及u^{-ℓ+s-3}ln u、u^{-ℓ+s-4}ln^2 u（高阶修正）。给出了引力扰动(s=-2)的具体尾系数。

Conclusion: 该工作系统给出了Kerr时空中线性场扰动推迟格林函数的高阶晚期尾行为，包括对数修正项，为理解黑洞晚期响应提供了更精确的渐近公式，并附带计算代码便于推广。

Abstract: We calculate high-order late-time tails of the retarded Green function of the Teukolsky equation for linear field perturbations of (subextremal) Kerr spacetime. We calculate these tails at a fixed spheroidal harmonic $\ell$ and azimuthal number $m$ up to the first three orders for the field point: at finite radius (away from the event horizon) for large Boyer-Lindquist time $t$; along the future event horizon $\mathscr{H}^+$ for large ingoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinate $v$; and along future null infinity $\mathscr{I}^+$ for large outgoing Eddington-Finkelstein coordinate $u$. We obtain the tail powers for generic integer field spin $s$ and the tail coefficients specifically for gravitational ($s=-2$) perturbations. Our asymptotics include the known leading power-law (generic) tails, respectively,$t^{-2\ell-3}$, $e^{imΩ_H v}v^{-2\ell-3-b}$ (where $b=1$ for $s>0, m=0$ and $b=0$ otherwise, and where $Ω_H$ is the angular velocity of the event horizon) and $u^{-\ell+s-2}$, as well as their higher-order logarithmic corrections: $t^{-2\ell-5}\ln t$, $e^{imΩ_H v}v^{-2\ell-5-b}\ln v$ and $u^{-\ell+s-3}\ln u$ (as well as $u^{-\ell+s-4}\ln^2 u$). Since we obtain the high-order expansions for modes for generic $\ell$ and $m$, we can readily infer the explicit expansions of the {\it full} retarded Green function for $s=-2$ (and its decay powers for generic integer $s$). We obtain the late-time asymptotics from small-frequency expansions of the Fourier modes of the retarded Green function in the frequency domain. Accordingly, we also provide small-frequency expansions of various quantities of interest in the scattering theory. We also attach two notebooks which provide expansions for specific values of $s$: one notebook provides them to the first three leading orders for generic $\ell$ and the other one to arbitrary order for specific values of $\ell$.

</details>


### [158] [High-Frequency Gravitational Wave Constraints from Precision Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12617)
*Dmitry Budker,Valerie Domcke,Joachim Kopp,Oleg Tretiak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究利用激光腔中电磁波传播受引力波影响的效应，通过光学精密光谱学在100 kHz至100 MHz频率范围内搜索高频引力波，首次对该频段大部分区域设定了限制，并讨论了未来将灵敏度提升8个数量级、频率覆盖扩展至至少1 GHz的改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频引力波（100 kHz-100 MHz）是引力波探测中尚未被充分探索的频段，现有方法难以覆盖，因此需要发展新的探测技术来填补这一空白。

Method: 利用引力波对激光腔中电磁波传播的影响，通过调制发射光子频率的光学精密光谱学技术进行探测。

Result: 首次在100 kHz至100 MHz的大部分频段内对高频引力波设定了限制。

Conclusion: 该技术有望通过未来改进将灵敏度提升8个数量级，并将频率覆盖范围扩展至至少1 GHz。

Abstract: Gravitational waves affect the propagation of electromagnetic waves in laser cavities, modulating the frequency of emitted photons. We use this effect to search for high-frequency gravitational waves between 100 kHz and 100 MHz using optical precision spectroscopy. Our limits constrain much of this frequency range for the first time. We discuss future improvements of the technique, which we expect to enhance the sensitivity by eight orders of magnitude, and to extend the frequency coverage up to at least 1 GHz.

</details>


### [159] [Two-fluid $f$-mode oscillations of dark-matter-admixed neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12661)
*Zi-Yue Zheng,Ting-Ting Sun,Huan Chen,Xiao-Ping Zheng,Jin-Biao Wei,G. F. Burgio,H. -J. Schulze*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在全广义相对论框架下研究了暗物质混合中子星(DANS)的四极f-模振荡，通过求解双流体极向扰动方程（含引力波辐射边界条件）得到复本征频率，发现中等暗物质分数下双流体分支之一可变为弱辐射态，阻尼时间提升数个量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全GR框架下研究暗物质混合中子星的f-模振荡，超越此前仅依赖Cowling近似（固定度规扰动）的研究，通过保留度规扰动和辐射边界条件来更准确地描述双流体系统的引力波辐射特性。

Method: 采用微观Brueckner-Hartree-Fock物质匹配Shen2020壳层描述普通物质，冷自相互作用费米子流体描述暗物质（仅通过引力耦合）；对固定暗物质分数序列求解极向双流体扰动方程（含出射引力波边界条件），得到复本征频率；利用组分动能、位移重叠度、物质四极矩抵消诊断模式特征。

Result: 频谱包含两条主f-类序列，其局域特征可为普通物质主导、暗物质主导或混合态；中等暗物质分数下，双流体分支之一可变为弱辐射态，阻尼时间提升数个量级；给出了出射Zerilli振幅和引力波阻尼时间，并估算了达到指定探测器阈值所需的引力波能量。

Conclusion: 本研究通过保留度规扰动和辐射边界条件，将此前双流体Cowling研究扩展到更完整的形式，为暗物质混合中子星的引力波观测提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study quadrupolar $f$-mode oscillations of dark-matter-admixed neutron stars (DANSs) in full general relativity (GR). The ordinary component is described by microscopic Brueckner-Hartree-Fock matter matched to the Shen2020 crust, while the dark matter (DM) component is treated as a cold self-interacting fermion fluid coupled to ordinary matter only by gravity. For fixed-DM-fraction sequences we solve the polar two-fluid perturbation equations with an outgoing gravitational-wave (GW) boundary condition, obtaining complex eigenfrequencies rather than only real mode frequencies. The spectrum contains two principal $f$-like sequences. Their local character can be ordinary-matter-led, DM-led, or mixed, and is diagnosed using the component kinetic energies, the displacement overlap, and the cancellation of the matter quadrupole. A main result is that, for intermediate DM fractions, one of the two-fluid branches can become weakly radiating, with damping times enhanced by several orders of magnitude. The same calculation gives the outgoing Zerilli amplitude and the GW damping time, which we use to estimate the GW energy required to reach a prescribed detector threshold. Thus the analysis extends previous two-fluid Cowling studies by retaining metric perturbations and the radiative boundary condition.

</details>


### [160] [Evaluating KAGRA upgrade scenarios for multimessenger observations of binary neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12769)
*Yuta Michimura,Soichiro Morisaki,Kenta Hotokezaka,Masaomi Tanaka*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个计算高效的框架，结合Fisher矩阵估计、探测器占空因子和双中子星并合率，评估KAGRA探测器升级方案在多信使天文学中的性能。结果表明：高频升级可提升约20%的定位精度，但宽带升级能产生更多精确定位的事件；加入KAGRA后，在10^3 Mpc^^3体积内定位的事件数增加约60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双中子星并合是多信使天文学的关键目标，需要对引力波探测器进行升级。KAGRA正考虑宽带灵敏度提升（增加探测距离）和高频优化（关注中子星物理）两种方案。需要一种量化方法来评估不同升级方案对多信使观测性能的影响。

Method: 提出一个计算高效的框架，将Fisher矩阵估计的定位面积和定位体积估计，与探测器占空因子及双中子星并合率相结合，评估探测器升级方案在多信使网络中的性能。应用于KAGRA在LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA网络中的升级场景。

Result: 1）对于相同源，高频升级相比宽带升级可将天区定位精度提升约20%；2）考虑探测率后，宽带升级能产生更多精确定位的事件；3）尽管KAGRA的双中子星探测距离比其他探测器短，其加入使在10^3 Mpc^3体积内定位的事件数增加约60%。

Conclusion: 该框架为从多信使观测角度定量评估未来探测器升级方案提供了量化工具，帮助权衡定位精度与事件探测率之间的取舍。

Abstract: Binary neutron star mergers are key targets for multimessenger astronomy, motivating future upgrades of gravitational-wave detectors. For KAGRA, both broadband sensitivity improvements that increase the binary neutron star detection range, and high-frequency optimizations targeting neutron-star physics are under consideration. We present a computationally efficient framework to evaluate the multimessenger performance of detector upgrades by combining Fisher-matrix estimates of localization area and localization volume with detector duty factors and binary neutron star merger rates. We apply this framework to proposed KAGRA upgrade scenarios within the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA network. For identical sources, the high-frequency upgrade improves sky localization by about 20% compared with the broadband option. However, when detection rates are taken into account, the broadband upgrade yields a larger number of well-localized events. Despite its shorter binary neutron star range than the other detectors, the inclusion of KAGRA increases the number of events localized within $10^3$ Mpc$^3$ volume by about 60%. These results provide a quantitative framework for evaluating future detector upgrades from the perspective of multimessenger observations.

</details>


### [161] [Qualitative Analysis of Cosmological Models Using Dynamical System Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12791)
*Sayantan Ghosh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本论文系统研究了宇宙加速膨胀的理论与观测方面，涵盖暗能量模型与修正引力框架。通过分析多种模型（包括耗散Chaplygin气体、DBI暗能量、标量场等）在f(Q)引力及其他修正引力框架下的动力学行为、摄动稳定性和观测约束，评估其描述宇宙加速膨胀的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙加速膨胀的物理本质是当代宇宙学核心问题。现有ΛCDM模型面临巧合问题、精细调节问题等理论困境，因此需要探索暗能量模型和修正引力理论作为替代方案，并系统评估其动力学可行性和观测兼容性。

Method: 论文采用多方法结合的研究路径：1）利用宇宙学观测数据（CC、Pantheon+SH0ES、DESI）通过卡方检验和MCMC方法约束模型参数；2）采用高斯过程从观测数据中重构暗能量动力学；3）运用动力系统理论分析背景和摄动层次的相空间行为；4）推导规范不变摄动方程评估稳定性。

Result: 各章节分别给出了：1）f(Q)引力中耗散Chaplygin气体的观测约束及信息准则评估；2）基于DESI数据重构的DBI暗能量势函数及其参数约束；3）f(Q)引力中标量场宇宙学的动力系统分析结果；4）背景与摄动层次的扩展相空间动力学特征。

Conclusion: 论文系统评估了多种暗能量和修正引力模型在描述宇宙加速膨胀方面的可行性，为未来研究提供了理论基础和方法框架。附录部分补充了f(Q)引力场方程推导及相关基础问题的数学细节。

Abstract: This thesis investigates theoretical and observational aspects of cosmic acceleration, focusing on dark energy models and modified gravity frameworks. The goal is to analyse their viability, dynamical behaviour, and perturbative stability to identify models capable of describing the accelerated expansion of the Universe. Chapter 1 reviews the essential background General Relativity, and mathematics of teleparallel and symmetric teleparallel geometries, basic cosmological models, matter components, and the key observational probes. The chapter concludes with a summary of modified gravity theories, including f(R), f(T) and f(Q). Chapter 2 studies a dissipative Chaplygin gas cosmology in f(Q) gravity. The model is constrained using the CC and Pantheon+SH0ES dataset, and its performance is assessed through information criteria and diagnostic tools such as Om and statefinder analysis. Chapter 3 reconstructs the dynamics of Dirac-Born-Infeld (DBI) dark energy using Hubble and DESI observations via Gaussian Processes. The reconstructed potential is fitted to theoretical models using chi-square and MCMC techniques, giving constrains on DBI scalar field. Chapter 4 analyses canonical scalar-field cosmology in coincident f(Q) gravity using dynamical systems. Chapter 5 extends the dynamical analysis to the DBI scalar field in f(Q) gravity. Chapter 6 examines scalar-field evolution at both background and perturbation levels. Perturbation equations for key gauge invariant variables are derived, and an extended phase space combining background and perturbation dynamics is constructed. Finally, chapter 7 summarizes the main results along with scope for future research. Further mathematical details including derivation of field equation and cosmology equations and foundational issues related to f(Q) gravity are compiled in the following four sections: Appendix A, Appendix B, Appendix C and Appendix D.

</details>


### [162] [The entropy of dynamical black holes deviating from electrovacuum in second order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12794)
*Wen-Tao Fu,Ming-Fei Ji,Yu-Sen Zhou,Li-Ming Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论中，通过协变相空间形式论和微扰方法，研究了动力黑洞的熵与表观视界面积的关系。在零能量条件和渐近平稳条件下，熵到二阶与表观视界面积成正比并满足第二定律；否则需要额外假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论中，动力黑洞的熵是否仍与视界面积成正比，以及在偏离稳态的情况下第二定律如何成立，是该研究要回答的核心问题。

Method: 在稳态电真空背景（具有分叉 Killing 视界）上，使用高斯零坐标微扰构造表观视界，并计算其二阶面积修正；在协变相空间形式论中引入修正的规范能量，将外部物质贡献纳入，并通过平衡律将其与熵的二阶变分自然关联。

Result: 在零能量条件和渐近平稳条件下，动力黑洞的熵在二阶微扰下与表观视界面积成正比，且满足第二定律；若放弃零能量条件，则需引入额外假设才能建立该面积律。

Conclusion: 动力黑洞熵与表观视界面积的比例关系在特定条件下成立，为零能量条件在黑洞热力学中的关键作用提供了支持，也为更一般情况下的面积律研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We study the entropy of dynamical black holes in Einstein--Maxwell theory. Starting from a stationary electrovacuum background with a bifurcate Killing horizon, the apparent horizon is constructed perturbatively in Gaussian null coordinates, and the area of its cross section is derived to second order. Within the covariant phase space formalism, we introduce a modified canonical energy that incorporates contributions from external matter and is naturally related to the second-order variation of the entropy through the balance law. Under the null energy condition and the asymptotic stationary condition, the entropy is proportional to the area of the apparent horizon up to second-order and satisfies the second law. Without the null energy condition, establishing this area law requires additional assumptions.

</details>


### [163] [Prism Effect in Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13012)
*Aliasghar Parvizi,Tomasz Pawłowski*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个电磁场与量子背景几何相互作用的广义框架，采用扩展的玻恩-奥本海默近似，导出弯曲时空中电磁波传播的准现象学模型。该模型自然复现了与非线性光学中光-物质相互作用类似的色散效应，并在平坦量子FLRW背景上分析了可观测的棱镜行为特征，且适用于所有能量区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁波色散关系的修正是探测量子引力效应的核心探针，现有半经典方法在处理模式依赖的色散关系时存在局限，需要构建更普适的框架来描述光与量子几何的相互作用。

Method: 采用扩展的玻恩-奥本海默近似，构建电磁场与量子背景几何相互作用的一般框架，导出准现象学模型；结合解析技术与数值模拟，在平坦量子FLRW背景上分析电磁波传播。

Result: 该框架自然复现了类非线性光学中的色散效应，在量子FLRW背景上展示了量子光-几何相互作用导致的棱镜行为，且在所有能量区间均有效，超越了半经典极限。

Conclusion: 该框架为探测量子引力修正提供了超越半经典极限的理论工具，通过类棱镜色散效应可在所有能量区间获取可观测的量子引力信号。

Abstract: Modifications to the dispersion relation of electromagnetic (EM) waves are a central probe in the search for quantum gravitational effects. In this work, we construct a general framework for the interaction between the EM field and a quantum background geometry, employing an extended Born-Oppenheimer approximation. This leads to a quasi-phenomenological model for EM wave propagation in curved spacetime. Unlike previous semi-classical approaches for mode-dependent dispersion relations, our framework naturally reproduces chromatic dispersion effects analogous to those observed in light-matter interactions in nonlinear optics. As a concrete application, we analyze EM wave propagation on a flat quantum Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) background, combining analytical techniques with numerical simulations to extract observable signatures of the prism-like behavior induced by quantum light-geometry interactions. Crucially, it remains valid across all energy regimes, enabling access to quantum gravitational corrections beyond the semi-classical limit.

</details>


### [164] [Antikick Relation in High-Energy Head-On Collisions of Spinning Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13018)
*Carlos O. Lousto,James Healy,Alessandro Ciarfella,Hiroyuki Nakano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟研究等质量自旋黑洞对头对头相对论性碰撞，分析了反冲速度的峰值、终值及反冲下降现象，发现相对论区域峰值与终值反冲呈比例关系（V_p≈7.4V_f），并建立了反冲对初始动量和自旋的唯象拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索黑洞在极端动力学区域（高能头对头碰撞）中的引力反冲行为，特别是自旋和初始动量对反冲结构的影响，填补此前主要关注擦边碰撞而头对头碰撞反冲细节研究的空白。

Method: 使用完整数值相对论模拟，对自旋大小s=0.5,0.65,0.8且初始动量γv变化的等质量黑洞头对头碰撞序列进行计算；结合零频极限（ZFL）分析辐射能量和动量、准正则模式（QNM）模型描述反冲下降，以及叠加Boosted双Kerr近距离极限估计。

Result: 发现相对论区域（γv>1）峰值反冲V_p与终值反冲V_f呈正比关系V_p≈7.4V_f（等价于ΔV≈-6.4V_f），几乎独立于初始动量和自旋大小；ZFL预测反冲振幅的主导项为线性自旋依赖，近距离极限分析预测s^3主导，而数值结果的经验指数为s^{1.27±0.08}。

Conclusion: 头对头高能碰撞反冲具有普适的峰终值比例关系，暗示碰撞后弛豫过程的共性；现有自旋依赖关系介于ZFL和近距离极限预测之间，需更高能量碰撞序列进一步确定。

Abstract: The collision of black holes at relativistic speeds probes gravity in its most extreme dynamical regime. While the maximum gravitational recoil from \emph{grazing} high-energy collisions ($\approx28\,562$~km/s, i.e., $\sim0.1c$) and the maximum radiated energy $E_{\rm rad}$ and remnant spin $α_f^{\max}$ from such encounters ($E_{\rm rad}/M_{\rm ADM}\approx32\%$ where $M_{\rm ADM}$ is the ADM mass, and $α_f^{\max}\approx0.987$) have been established previously~\cite{Healy:2022jbh,Healy:2024lhl}, here we focus on the \emph{head-on} high-energy collision of equal-mass spinning black holes and on the detailed structure of the resulting recoil. Performing a sequence of full numerical simulations for spin magnitudes $s=0.5,0.65$, and $0.8$ over a range of initial momenta $γv$, we characterize the peak recoil $V_p$, the final recoil $V_f$, and the antikick $ΔV\equiv V_f-V_p$, and we provide phenomenological fits of their dependence on $γv$ and $s$. We complement these results with a zero-frequency-limit (ZFL) analysis of the radiated energy and momentum, a quasinormal-mode model of the antikick, and a superposed boosted double-Kerr close-limit estimate. We find that in the relativistic regime ($γv>1$) the peak and final recoil are directly proportional, $V_p\approx7.4\,V_f$ (equivalently $ΔV \approx-6.4\,V_f$), largely independent of both the initial momentum and the spin magnitude, pointing to a common post-merger relaxation. While the ZFL predicts a leading linear-in-spin dependence, the close-limit analysis predicts a leading $s^3$ dependence of the recoil amplitude; with the three spin magnitudes studied here the empirical exponent is $s^{1.27\pm0.08}$, motivating an even higher energy collision spin sequence study.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Capillary prisms, Coxeter gluing, and the $π_2$-systole of 3-manifolds with positive scalar curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12305)
*Shihang He,Chao Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文通过局部到整体的方法研究正数量曲率三维流形的π₂-收缩，利用毛细棱柱的能量本质性获得了角度敏感的π₂-收缩下界估计，并建立了Coxeter粘合与光滑化定理，最终在连通和拓扑上构造了具有大π₂-收缩的光滑正数量曲率度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正数量曲率三维流形的π₂-收缩下界，并建立Coxeter多面体光滑化原理的严格数学框架，该原理长期以来在正数量曲率几何中被启发式地使用。

Method: 采用局部到整体的方法：局部使用毛细棱柱作为砖块（tessellated building blocks），通过准局部质量的单调性得到角度敏感的π₂-收缩估计；整体步骤通过Coxeter粘合将毛细棱柱复制品粘合，并在满足Coxeter相容性和平均曲率跳跃条件下证明光滑化定理以保持标量曲率下界。

Result: 获得了毛细棱柱上角度敏感的π₂-收缩估计：当上毛细角≤α∈(0,π/2]时，sys₂(M,∂₀M,g)·inf R_g ≲ |log α|⁻¹；建立了Coxeter粘合与光滑化定理；在S²×S¹和透镜空间的连通和上构造了具有大π₂-收缩的光滑正数量曲率度量。

Conclusion: 给出了首个在连通和拓扑上具有定量大π₂-收缩的显式度量，并讨论了局部π₂-收缩估计的刚性与非刚性现象。

Abstract: We study the $π_2$-systole of positive scalar curvature $3$-manifolds via a local-to-global approach. The tessellated local building blocks are capillary prisms $M$: Riemannian cylinders enclosed by mean convex surfaces meeting at prescribed dihedral angles. For a class of such prisms, called energy-essential prisms, we obtain angle-sensitive relative $π_2$-systole estimates. In particular, if the upper capillary angle is at most $α\in (0,\tfracπ{2}]$, then
  \[\mathrm{sys}_2(M,\partial_0 M, g)\cdot \inf R_g \lesssim |\log α|^{-1},\qquad α\to 0.\]
  The proof relies on the monotonicity of a new quasi-local mass.
  The local-to-global step is achieved via Coxeter gluing of copies of capillary prisms. We prove a general Coxeter gluing and smoothing theorem for scalar curvature: under Coxeter compatibility of the corner strata and nonnegative mean curvature jump conditions along the glued facets, we prove that the resulting piecewise smooth manifold can be smoothened while preserving the scalar curvature lower bound. This theorem gives, to our knowledge, the first general rigorous formulation and proof of the Coxeter-polyhedral smoothing principle for scalar curvature lower bounds, a principle that has long been used heuristically in positive scalar curvature geometry.
  As an application, we construct smooth positive scalar curvature metrics with large $π_2$-systole on connected sums of copies of $S^2\times S^1$ and lens spaces that are not covered by $S^2\times R$. These examples give the first explicit metrics with quantitatively large $π_2$-systole on these topologies. We also discuss rigidity and non-rigidity phenomena for the local $π_2$-systolic estimates.

</details>


### [166] [Submetries in non-positive signature and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12516)
*Matteo Zanardini*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出了时空背景下子度量（submetry）概念的推广，证明了在适当完备性假设下，光滑时空之间的洛伦兹子度量对应于局部C^{1,1}半黎曼淹没，并探讨了其在类时曲率界、等距群作用、非因果叶状结构和洛伦兹-瓦瑟斯坦几何中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典子度量概念在黎曼几何中研究充分，但在洛伦兹几何（时空）背景下缺乏系统推广。本文旨在建立时空版本的子度量理论，填补该空白。

Method: 利用洛伦兹几何的因果结构，将子度量的定义适配到带洛伦兹度量的时空中；通过完备性假设和正则性分析，建立其与半黎曼淹没的等价关系。

Result: 光滑时空间的洛伦兹子度量在完备性条件下等价于局部C^{1,1}半黎曼淹没。

Conclusion: 该理论可应用于类时曲率界、等距群作用、非因果叶状结构和洛伦兹-瓦瑟斯坦几何等方向，展示了洛伦兹子度量的广泛适用性。

Abstract: We consider a generalization of the notion of submetry tailored to the setting of spacetimes. We show that, under suitable completeness assumptions, Lorentzian submetries between smooth spacetimes correspond to locally C^{1,1} Semi-Riemannian submersions. Moreover, we establish some applications regarding timelike curvature bounds, isometric group actions, acausal foliations and Lorentz-Wasserstein geometry.

</details>


### [167] [Sard property for rank 2 polarizations in metabelian Lie groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12530)
*Enrico Le Donne,Antonio Lerario,Luca Nalon,Nicola Paddeu,Luca Rizzi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了亚贝尔李群上秩2极化的异常集维数界限，证明了该类群端点映射的Sard性质。同时得到了导出子群余维数不超过2的亚贝尔李群上Goh-异常集的维数界限，进而推断这些带次黎曼结构的极化群满足极小化Sard性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立亚贝尔李群上端点映射的Sard性质，并推广到更广泛的情形（导出子群余维数≤2的亚贝尔李群），为次黎曼几何中的极小化问题提供理论基础。

Method: 通过分析秩2极化下的异常集维数界限，以及导出子群余维数≤2条件下Goh-异常集的维数界限，结合极化群与次黎曼结构的关联进行推导。

Result: 得到了秩2极化下异常集的维数界限，以及导出子群余维数≤2时Goh-异常集的维数界限。

Conclusion: 这些极化群在次黎曼结构下满足极小化Sard性质。

Abstract: We provide bounds on the dimension of the abnormal set for rank 2 polarizations on metabelian Lie groups, establishing the Sard property for the end-point map of such groups. We also obtain bounds for the dimension of the Goh-abnormal set for metabelian Lie groups where the codimension of the derived subgroup is at most 2, with no assumption on the rank of the polarization. We thus infer that these polarized groups, equipped with sub-Riemannian structures, satisfy the minimizing Sard property.

</details>


### [168] [Lectures on Alexandrov spaces with curvature bounded below](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12842)
*Vitali Kapovitch,Nina Lebedeva,Anton Petrunin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文是Alexandrov空间（曲率下有界）的综述性导论，涵盖了比较条件、整体化定理、切空间与方向空间、梯度流、分裂定理、维数与体积、Gromov选择定理、边界与加倍定理、商空间，以及作为前驱的二维理论概述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为读者系统介绍Alexandrov空间的基本理论框架，梳理该领域核心结果与工具，填补几何分析中相关知识的综述空白。

Method: 采用综述性论述方法，依次阐述曲率下有界的Alexandrov空间的多个关键定理和概念，包括比较条件、整体化、切空间构造、梯度流分析、分裂定理、维数体积性质、Gromov选择定理、边界加倍定理、商空间，并回顾二维理论作为历史背景。

Result: 系统呈现了Alexandrov空间理论的主要组成部分：比较条件与整体化定理建立了全局曲率约束；切空间与方向空间刻画了局部结构；梯度流提供了分析工具；分裂定理、维数体积性质、Gromov选择定理揭示了空间的几何与拓扑不变量；边界与加倍定理扩展了空间类别；商空间展示了等距群作用下的结构保持；二维理论为现代理论提供了基础。

Conclusion: Alexandrov空间理论是一个丰富而自洽的几何框架，通过曲率下有界条件统一了多个核心几何结果，并为后续研究（如度量几何、Ricci曲率流等）提供了坚实基础。二维理论是其重要历史先导。

Abstract: An introduction to Alexandrov spaces with curvature bounded below. Topics include various comparison conditions, the globalization theorem, tangent spaces and spaces of directions, gradient flows, the splitting theorem, dimension and volume, Gromov's selection theorem, the boundary and the doubling theorem, and quotient spaces. We also give a brief overview of the two-dimensional theory, the main precursor to modern Alexandrov geometry.

</details>


### [169] [Comparison Theorems for Fractional GJMS Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12859)
*Huihuang Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究Poincaré-Einstein流形共形边界上的分数阶GJMS算子，推导了分数阶Yamabe常数的两个比较不等式，并由此得到刚性定理，部分验证了分数阶Yamabe常数的单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索分数阶GJMS算子P_{2γ}对应的分数阶Yamabe常数之间的比较关系，并验证其单调性猜想。

Method: 针对γ∈(1/2,1)和γ∈(1,2)两种情形，分别建立P_1与P_{2γ}、P_2与P_{2γ}之间的比较不等式，并通过等式成立的条件刻画刚性定理。

Result: 得到了两个比较不等式及其对应的刚性定理，结合已有结果部分支持了分数阶Yamabe常数的单调性。

Conclusion: 为分数阶Yamabe常数关于γ的单调性提供了部分理论证据。

Abstract: In this paper, we mainly focus on the fractional GJMS operators $P_{2γ}$ which are defined on the conformal infinity of a Poincaré-Einstein manifold. We derive two comparison inequalities of the fractional Yamabe constants associated to the fractional GJMS operators. One is between $P_{1}$ and $P_{2γ}$ for $γ\in (1/2,1)$, and the other is between $P_{2}$ and $P_{2γ}$ for $γ\in (1,2)$. They both imply the rigidity theorems by characterizing the equalities. Together with the result in \cite{WZ1}, we partially provide some evidence for the monotonicity of the fractional Yamabe constants.

</details>


### [170] [Ambient geometry via min-max widths of embedded circles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12979)
*Lucas Ambrozio,Ivan Miranda,Rafael Montezuma*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了黎曼球面的Birkhoff极小极大不变量的下界，并通过诱导扫除方法建立了嵌入圆的极小极大宽度与球面扫除之间的联系；同时给出了非紧完备黎曼流形中嵌入圆宽度上界与Urysohn维数理论中1-宽度的估计，以及欧几里得平面中经典宽度与极小极大宽度的下界关系，最后证明了Zoll球面中圆球面的刚性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立黎曼球面Birkhoff极小极大不变量与嵌入圆极小极大宽度之间的定量关系，并推广到非紧完备流形和欧几里得平面，最终刻画圆球面在Zoll球面中的刚性特征。

Method: 通过从球面闭曲线扫除诱导出嵌入圆上点对的扫除，使得每对点位于周围扫除的邻近曲线中；利用Urysohn定量维数理论建立流形到有限图的连续函数与直径一致有界的水平集之间的联系。

Result: 得到了黎曼球面Birkhoff极小极大不变量的下界；非紧完备流形中嵌入圆宽度上界与1-宽度的估计关系；欧几里得平面中闭简单曲线经典宽度的下界；以及Zoll球面中圆球面的刚性刻画。

Conclusion: 本文的诱导扫除方法和维数理论估计为极小极大理论提供了新工具，并揭示了圆球面在Zoll球面中的唯一性。

Abstract: We prove a lower bound for the Birkhoff min-max invariant of a Riemannian sphere in terms of the min-max width of its embedded circles. The main tool is a method to induce a sweepout by pairs of points in an embedded circle from a given sweepout of the sphere by closed curves, so that the two points of each pair of the induced sweepout lie in nearby curves of the ambient sweepout. Moreover, considering a non-compact complete Riemannian manifold, we relate upper bounds for the min-max width of its embedded circles to the existence of continuous functions from the manifold to finite graphs with level sets that have uniformly bounded diameters, thus giving an estimate for its $1$-width in Urysohn's quantitative dimension theory. In the specific case of the Euclidean plane, we bound from below the classical width of a closed simple curve by its min-max width. Finally, we prove related rigidity results characterizing the round spheres among Zoll spheres.

</details>
